> *(Trailblazer)* We need to go after that black-robed swordmaster.
> *(Trailblazer)* Trianne... I have a bad feeling about this.

**Phainon:** That you're both safe is already a blessing.

**Phainon:** Everyone, we must take action now. We should split into two groups: One group will stay in the Abyss and meet up with Aglaea's reinforcements, then search for Trianne's whereabouts.

**Phainon:** The other group will come with me to pursue the black-robed swordmaster and recover the Coreflame.

**Dan Heng:** Do you know where it's headed?

**Phainon:** I didn't get to tell you this before, but Anaxa and I already have our misgivings about the black tide, so we've made preparations.

**Phainon:** I'll tell you the details as we move. Opportunities are fleeting on the battlefield. We should hurry.

**Okhema Scout:** Lord Phainon! Ah... Lady Tribbie! And Lady Trinnon! I'm glad you're alright!

**Phainon:** Did Aglaea's people get here?

**Okhema Scout:** Yes! Lady Aglaea already knows about the situation and sent along a message.

**Okhema Scout:** "Phainon, I understand that swordmaster is of great importance to you, but if you charge in without a plan, I fear that... you won't be the only one who dies in vain."

**Phainon:** ...I understand. "Hatred is a hero's deadliest poison." I have no intention of fighting alone. If that swordmaster really is related to the black tide, the Chrysos Heirs must not take them lightly.

**Phainon:** I have indeed planned to return to the holy city and discuss the strategy with Aglaea. We'll need all the help we can get for this encirclement.

**Dan Heng:** Why don't we split up as previously discussed? Hyacine and I will stay to regroup with the reinforcements. You go back to the holy city with everyone else and tell Aglaea about your plan.

**Trinnon:** Trinnon will stay in the Abyss, too. That way, both sides can communicate with each other if anything happens.

**Phainon:** Thank you, everyone. (Trailblazer), Tribbie, let's go.

### Part 81 — `databank/missions/chapter-02-light-slips.md`
**Context:** Mission 9: Passage, Reveal the Past Once More > Tribios' Last Conversation with Oronyx
**Kind:** personal
**Id:** `chapter-02-light-slips__tribios-last-conversation-with-oronyx`

**Oronyx:** Tri... bios...

**Tribios:** You're here, Oronyx.

**Oronyx:** You still... consume yourself... for that false prophecy.

**Tribios:** "False prophecy," huh? So you still think that way.

**Oronyx:** Even if Kephale has fallen... they would never guide humans to kill their own comrades. Stop while you still can. Tribios... Spreading the prophecy will only kindle disaster. That disaster... will tear you into a thousand pieces.

**Tribios:** But the black tide has already arrived, and Janus remains in slumber. If even the god of passages, known for guiding mortals, cannot resist this catastrophe, then what can we do against the incoming destruction?

**Oronyx:** Do not... get involved with the Coreflames. If you do that... You can still remain chosen by fate.

**Tribios:** Those chosen by fate would never have their family taken from them. And how can I alone remain safe and secure, while watching the entire world crumble before my eyes? This might be our last conversation, Oronyx. I will do everything I can... to protect you from the black tide.

---

### Part 82 — `databank/missions/chapter-02-light-slips.md`
**Context:** Mission 9: Passage, Reveal the Past Once More > The Night Before
**Kind:** personal
**Id:** `chapter-02-light-slips__the-night-before`

*(Young Tribios with her mother, Mortis.)*

**Tribios:** Mama... are you going to that ritual tomorrow?

**Gentle Mother:** That's right, my sweet girl. What's the matter?

**Tribios:** I wish I could go too... I have something to ask Janus. I want to know how to chase the black tide away!

**Gentle Mother:** Ah, I see. Unfortunately, it's a secret ritual, and only I can preside over it. If you want me to consult the Titan on your behalf... you'll have to promise me something. Be a good girl and go to sleep now, and whatever you dream of, don't be afraid— Just sleep through the night, until morning comes, until the next time Aquila opens their eyes. Can you do that for me, sweetie?

**Tribios:** That's it? Of course I can! Tribios is the most amazing Holy Maiden of Janus... Well, little Holy Maiden! But... why, Mama?

**Gentle Mother:** Because... this ritual might take a long time.

**Tribios:** How long?

**Gentle Mother:** Tomorrow, my sweet child. I'll be back tomorrow. If not tomorrow, then the next tomorrow. But if I still haven't returned for a very long time... That means the ritual has succeeded. It means I've found a way to chase away the black tide and reached the new world on the other side. But for various reasons, I can't return to bring my sweet girl with me.

**Gentle Mother:** But I'll be waiting for you there. As long as you stay brave and kind, we'll meet again in that new world.

**Gentle Mother:** It's the end of the west wind, the black tide's other shore, a radiant sea of flowers blessed and protected by the gods. In the rosy horizon, you'll see a silver-white shoal. It marks the end of your voyage — a haven free from storms, cold, and heavy rains. No sorrow can linger there... That is where I'll be waiting for you.

**Tribios:** Wow! That sounds just like the island from my dream! Oh, I know! I'll turn the moon into a ship and the stars into sails to go find Mama! Pinky promise?

**Gentle Mother:** Mmm, pinky promise. As long as you sail toward tomorrow, as long as the sun will rise for the next day... Our wishes will come true together.

---

### Part 83 — `databank/missions/chapter-02-light-slips.md`
**Context:** Mission 9: Passage, Reveal the Past Once More > Tribios Claims the Coreflame
**Kind:** personal
**Id:** `chapter-02-light-slips__tribios-claims-the-coreflame`

*(Tribios enters the Janus Vault.)*

**Tribios:** At long last...

**Janus:** Tribios... you've come.

**Tribios:** ...I knew there was some sanity left in you, Janus. Just as I thought... you too have been confined.

**Janus:** Step forth, and thou shalt bury yourself... Bury the gods, and bury the fate that binds us all.

**Tribios:** No, Janus... I've come here... to take on your fate.

**Tribios:** By the blood of the lamb and the strength of my blade-wielding right hand... I am Tribios, the Listener, the announcer, and the witness of the prophecy — I, the Holy Maiden of Janusopolis, the walker of the infinite paths, have come forth to assume your duty.

**Janus:** Hehe... The one who guides the world shall see their soul shattered into a thousand fragments, akin to glass crashing upon the ground...

**Tribios:** Just as I wished. This journey promises to be a long one. With a thousand selves to accompany me, I'll never be lonely.

**Janus:** Though thou bearest the Coreflame, thou shalt be far from the glory of divinity. "Passages" are forever associated with dirt, and even a mere mortal's blade can imperil thy very existence...

**Tribios:** No matter. My hands are not made for bearing arms, but my feet are destined to embark on a journey for the benefit of all.

**Janus:** Yet, the Flame-Chase is a journey of constant loss, among which even life itself holds little value... Time decays the heart, the passages shatter the body, and the scales weigh the separation, until the end draws near... The one who bears three lives... art thou truly prepared?

**Tribios:** Without a doubt, Titan. Let the prophecy shatter me — so that I may open a myriad of paths for this imperiled world!

**Janus:** Thus... it shall be...

*(The dying Titan's prophecy:)*

**Dying Titan:** Overthrow the gods — / Return the Coreflames — Inherit divine authority — / Forge godly miracles — Go kill — / Go mourn — Kill my kin — / Mourn our fate — Ferry the spirits of the gods — / Nourish the land with divine essence —

**Dying Titan:** Yet, thou shalt remember — **All shall bid farewell to one, and that person alone will witness the miracle —** Such... is destiny —

**Tribios:** I understand, Janus... Thank you. Now... may you rest well.

**Tribios:** Mother... Did you... see that? I have assumed my duty and stand ready to set out... We will step over the gods' corpses and wander among humanity... And thus, we shall sweep apart Amphoreus' pitch-dark fog... May we... May the world... Follow the myriad paths trodden and reunite at the end of the west wind... where flowers bloom in spring. Mother... I shall see you tomorrow.

---

*(Guards arrive. Tribios escapes, aided by a mysterious force — which is revealed to be her future self, reaching back through time.)*

---

### Part 84 — `databank/missions/chapter-02-light-slips.md`
**Context:** Mission 9: Passage, Reveal the Past Once More > The Letter Across Time
**Kind:** collective-pointer
**Id:** `chapter-02-light-slips__the-letter-across-time`
**Collective:** `databank/memories/collective/chapter-02-light-slips.md`
**Witness:** spoke

**Tribios:** The answer is already within you, and you've made your own decision.

**Phainon:** ...What? You can... hear us?

**Trinnon:** Not really... She can neither see nor hear us. Yet, she knew that one day, someone would return to unveil this dust-covered memory.

**Trinnon:** We can never know for sure who helped us back then. But that kindness was forever engraved in our hearts, motivating us to move forward. Time has modified this memory into a special letter, so that we do not forget our original intent. It's a letter written by Tribios of the past and sent to her future self.

**Tribios:** Yes, you'll remember it all, and then find your way back to the gate where everything began. Here, I will set sail toward the distant horizon. Here, you have traveled far on your way home.

**Tribios:** Such is the maxim that unveils Tribios' fate: **"You shall shatter into a thousand fragments and wither on the soil of foreign lands."** So, if you ever feel lost, return to this place. I'll keep this memory here for you — or for me — to embrace it again.

**Trinnon:** Worry not. Though loss is something we've grown used to, our resolve and our pledge are what Tribios will never forget. Each one of us has never failed to remember.

**Tribios:** In the name of the Three Fates, I stand by my answer with no regrets. Now, with or without the gods' blessing... I run towards the world's agony. If I can truly pierce through the utter darkness... We will meet again at the end of the west wind where flowers bloom in spring.

---

*Light Calendar 3760, Month of Evernight: "Janusopolis' Holy Maiden" Tribios bore the Coreflame to quell the world's chaos and became a demigod of Janus. She traversed the "Gate of Infinity," splitting into a thousand messengers to spread the divine prophecy across the land of Amphoreus.*

*Light Calendar 3870, Month of Freedom: Humanity's Flame-Chase Journey officially began.*

### Part 85 — `databank/missions/chapter-02-light-slips.md`
**Context:** Mission 9: Passage, Reveal the Past Once More > The Promise of Tomorrow
**Kind:** collective-pointer
**Id:** `chapter-02-light-slips__the-promise-of-tomorrow`
**Collective:** `databank/memories/collective/chapter-02-light-slips.md`
**Witness:** spoke

**Castorice:** Lady Tribbie, do you still think... That everything will be fine tomorrow?

**Tribbie:** Yeah, that's what Trianne would say as well.

**Castorice:** With all due respect... Lady Tribbie, you know perfectly well... that "see you tomorrow" is nothing but a white lie. I hope your toughness is not a mask hiding a lonely heart.

**Tribbie:** Cas, that's not a lie. **"See you tomorrow"** is the greatest prophecy in the world.

**Castorice:** Prophecy...?

**Tribbie:** Listen. In every corner of this land, every heartbeat carries a prayer for that prophecy to come true. It may be that only a few heroes will bring tomorrow to the new world, but the prophecy of leading everyone to tomorrow belongs to all in Amphoreus.

**Tribbie:** Because people believe it will come true, and they are trying hard to hold onto that belief. Even as the world teeters on the brink of collapse, everyone is pressing forward, longing to reach farther and move toward tomorrow.

**Tribbie:** "Fate" is not about the outcome, but the journey. It's not the flowers that bloom at the end of the road... But rather the paths that lead beyond the gate, where people tread toward the sea of flowers.

**Tribbie:** So, Cas, if anything is troubling you, just set it aside for tomorrow. There's no difference between demigods and mortals. The troubles we encounter can only be resolved by our future selves. Reflect through the long night, wait for the dawn as you think, then set off at daybreak.

**Tribbie:** Because that's how Tribios has journeyed so far to spread the prophecy... Beckoning the morning sun... and the tomorrow it promises to bring.

---

**Trinnon:** (Trailblazer), and Mem, thank you. Your resolves cannot be questioned. We praise your pure selflessness and courage... May Kephale light your path forward.

**Tribbie:** Get ready to assume Oronyx's divinity, Trailblazer.

**Trinnon:** The divine position of Oronyx has already been vacant for some time... We can feel the space of the trial closing. As there isn't much of their soul left, we'll have to complete the ceremony before their soul completely dissipates.

> *(Trailblazer)* Let's begin. / *I've long gotten used to fighting fires.*

**Trinnon:** The eighth Coreflame coveted by the miracle, that once belonged to the great Veil of Evernight, Oronyx. It shall now be returned by the two elected heroes of the Chrysos Heirs: a traveler who is upright and courageous and a sacred beast that invokes miracles.

**Mem:** It's also "who" for me, not "that."

**Trinnon:** Both of you, please come forward. Recite along with us.

**Trinnon & (Trailblazer) & Mem:** "O majesty of the Twelve Titans, pillars of the world—"
**Trinnon & (Trailblazer) & Mem:** "We seek your divinity, to mend the rifts of the world —"
**Trinnon & (Trailblazer) & Mem:** "Fill our bodies with blood of gold, till we wither in willing service to the prophecy..."

**(Trailblazer):** I don't think I have gold blood?

**Trinnon:** ...These are just words meant to make the prayer seem more solemn. Please focus... ...and surrender the Coreflame.

*(Submit Coreflame of Time. The constellation representing Time is illuminated.)*

---

### Part 89 — `databank/missions/chapter-03-through-petals.md`
**Context:** Chapter 3 — Through the Petals in the Land of Repose (v3.2) > Sub-Mission 6: Pathstrider, Set Sail Upon the River of Souls > Speak with the Trianne Doll Again Before Heading Out
**Kind:** collective-pointer
**Id:** `chapter-03-through-petals__speak-with-the-trianne-doll-again-before-heading-out`
**Collective:** `databank/memories/collective/chapter-03-through-petals.md`
**Witness:** spoke

*(You've spent far too long searching for Thanatos. Now, the closer you seem to get to them, the longer the night seems to last. You still have some things to say to Trianne, and someone else seems to have noticed your thoughts.)*

*(Later, somewhere in Okhema...)*
*(narrative)* **Castorice:** ...
**Tribbie:** Cas? What a surprise.
**Castorice:** Lady Tribbie...?
**Tribbie:** Is something troubling you?
**Castorice:** ...I just wanted to speak to Lady Trianne for a bit before I set off.
**Castorice:** No matter how breathtakingly beautiful Styxia once was, the place is now the domain of the departed... There is no telling what we'll encounter there.
**Castorice:** I... don't want to have any regrets.
**Tribbie:** Has anyone told you that your eyes are sparkling today?
**Castorice:** Is that a figure of speech...?
**Tribbie:** In the past... When you did smile once in a while, all of us could see that your heart remained troubled even as you smiled.
**Castorice:** Lady Tribbie, you know perfectly well... that **see you tomorrow** is nothing but a white lie.
**Castorice:** I hope your toughness is not a mask hiding a lonely heart.
**Tribbie:** Cas... You hardly ever talked of your past. None of us knows what you went through apart from Agy. But we know it must have been filled with pain and hardships, as it is the fate every Chrysos Heir is destined to live out.
**Tribbie:** ...**We** are just happy for you that the Coreflame you're meant to inherit has finally surfaced.
**Castorice:** Isn't it a little too soon to say that?
**Tribbie:** But you already know where "tomorrow" is, don't you?
**Tribbie:** There is no need to rely on anyone else. Just give it your all and leave no regrets. **We** have always believed that your embrace is just as warm as everyone else's.
**Tribbie:** Because we embrace others to comfort them, and Cas... you're the kindest Chrysos Heir **we** know.
**Castorice:** ...Thank you, Lady Tribbie.
**Castorice:** This feeling... It's hard to put into words. I suddenly found myself with so many wishes... so much I want to accomplish before setting off.
**Tribbie:** Then go do it, Cas!
**Tribbie:** Trianne... was actually working on a surprise gift for you. It's a pity she didn't get to finish it, but now Trinnon and **I** are working on it, and we'll make sure to give it to you.
**Tribbie:** So, let's meet back here when you return from Styxia. Safe travels — see you tomorrow!

---

### Part 90 — `databank/missions/chapter-03-through-petals.md`
**Context:** Chapter 3 — Through the Petals in the Land of Repose (v3.2) > Sub-Mission 8: Ferryman, Ferry Me Across the Stream of Souls > Continue Exploring Dragonbone City and Proceed Upward with the Chains of the Past
**Kind:** collective-pointer
**Id:** `chapter-03-through-petals__continue-exploring-dragonbone-city-and-proceed-upward-with-the-chains-of`
**Collective:** `databank/memories/collective/chapter-03-through-petals.md`
**Witness:** spoke

**Castorice:** Is that...
**Castorice:** ...

*(Approach Trianne)*
**Trianne:** Wow, there are so many of you!
**Trianne:** It's Little Cas, and Little Gray! There's also... Little De and the pink puppy!
**Mem:** Trianne? Aren't you...

> *(Trailblazer)* ...Dead?
> *(Trailblazer)* Trianne and Mydei... I get it now.
> **Mem:** G-Get what? I'm getting even more confused...
> **Mydei:** Heh...
> **Mydei:** This is... the first time I've encountered a deceased friend while going upstream in the River of Souls.
> **Castorice:** It really... works like this.
> **Castorice:** Just like the other departed spirits we've encountered along the way... I'm also unable to sense the aura of the living on Lady Trianne.
> **Trianne:** Yup! You're right. Trianne already knows that she'll not be able to return to everyone's side.
> **Trianne:** But Trianne believed that Little Cas would eventually return to the nether realm...
> **Trianne:** So Trianne has been standing here, at this spot closest to the world of the living, and waiting for Little Cas!
> **Castorice:** ...
> **Castorice:** It must have been... very tough for you.
> **Mem:** But according to what Trianne said... We're almost at the entrance of the nether realm.
> **Trianne:** Trianne promised Tribbie and Trinnon that when Little Cas got here, Trianne would get the gift ready and be the first person to run over and welcome you.
> **Trianne:** So, Little Cas, don't cry! Please accept this...
> **Trianne:** ...**We** finally finished it!
> **Castorice:** This is what Lady Tribbie once mentioned...
> **Castorice:** The gift for me... that you didn't manage to finish...
> **Mydei:** The Holy Maiden of Janus... Even when separated by life and death, their thoughts are still connected.
> **Trianne:** Trianne spent a lot of time drawing this! Trianne hopes that Little Cas likes it.
> **Castorice:** Mmm... Seriously...
> **Castorice:** It's seriously... A wonderful drawing, Lady Trianne...
> **Mydei:** This is... our "Flame-Chase Journey"...
> **Trianne:** Little Cas, we don't know what your journey was like before. Tribbie has always said that it must have been a very harsh and frightening journey.
> **Trianne:** But Little Cas no longer has anything to fear now, because everyone is here with you in this Flame-Chase Journey!
> **Castorice:** That's right...
> **Castorice:** Only now, when I see Lady Trianne's gift, do I realize that I've already obtained so many things I would never have dared to dream of since I embarked on the Flame-Chase Journey.
> **Castorice:** It's the first time I've gained so many companions, the first time I picked up the hobbies of crafting and photography, the first time I had dinner with someone else, the first time I went to the market to buy the chimera doll I'd liked for a very long time and hugged it to sleep...
> **Castorice:** The first time I wore a dress personally made by Lady Aglaea... the first time I went for a walk with Lady Tribbie, Lady Trianne, and Lady Trinnon... the first time I enjoyed the night breeze and looked up at the stars with Miss Hyacine... the first time I took care of dromases with (Trailblazer)...
> **Castorice:** Looking back at all these experiences I never thought were possible... it feels as though the scales of my life had shrunk from centuries and decades down to minutes and seconds, and all of them are so precious...
> **Castorice:** ...
> **Castorice:** I took countless photos in the past, but they're all black-and-white landscapes. I don't have a single one with friends.
> **Castorice:** If I had known... I would have taken more pictures...

> *(Trailblazer)* Then shall we take one now?
> **Trianne:** Trianne says yes! Trianne likes Little Cas and would love to take a picture with Little Cas!
> **Mydei:** Sadly...
> **Mydei:** Being departed souls, our images cannot be captured in the world of the living.
> **Castorice:** ...I thought so, too.
> **Mem:** What? Are we seriously going to leave any room for regret?
> **Castorice:** There won't be any regrets...
> **Castorice:** This vivid drawing surpasses any photograph from the world of the living.
> **Trianne:** Little Cas smiled! Tribbie, Trinnon, and Trianne's wish has finally come true.
> **Castorice:** I remember you once said that fate is not the flowers that bloom at the end of the road, but where people tread beyond the gate... Now, the path that belongs to me is about to reach the end.
> **Castorice:** Thank you... for bringing me the final key.
> **Trianne:** Key? Little Cas, why are you talking in riddles all of a sudden?
> **Castorice:** I have a feeling that soon, this gift of yours will become the key to saving Miss (Trailblazer)'s life.
> **Trianne:** Wow! That's amazing! Then keep smiling and carry on bravely!
> **Trianne:** Little Cas, Little Gray, everyone — see you tomorrow!

*(With a blinding smile, Trianne's golden form disappears in the thin fog of Styxia's moonlit night.)*

**Castorice:** Lady Trianne...

### Part 93 — `databank/missions/chapter-04-fall-at-dawn.md`
**Context:** Chapter 4 — The Fall at Dawn's Rise (v3.3) > Mission 2 — Scrolls, Turn the Blade's Gaze > Meanwhile in Okhema — Aglaea's Final Moments
**Kind:** collective-pointer
**Id:** `chapter-04-fall-at-dawn__meanwhile-in-okhema-aglaea-s-final-moments`
**Collective:** `databank/memories/collective/chapter-04-fall-at-dawn.md`
**Witness:** spoke

> *Meanwhile, in Okhema, at the new Twilight Courtyard...*

**Hyacine:** Ah...

**Tribbie:** How's it going, Cinny? This place fits, doesn't it? It's a bit small, but the lighting is perfect!

**Hyacine:** Thank you, Lady Tribbie! With this, the Twilight Courtyard can be reborn in Okhema. Do you like it here too, Little Ica?

**Little Ica:** Doot... Doot-Doot!

**Tribbie:** Hehe... It's so bright here, maybe you should rename it? How about "Luminous Courtyard"?

**Hyacine:** Lady Tribbie makes a good point. But... "Twilight Courtyard" carries the long history of the descendants of the sky, and I'd like it to continue.

**Tribbie:** Right... How does the legend go again? Hmm...

**Hyacine:** Heh... Even Lady Tribbie forgets things...

**Tribbie:** ...We... We just haven't prepared lessons in a while now, so we're a bit rusty! Just give us some time, and we'll remember! ...Ah, now we remember! *Kremnos and the Firmament*, Volume 3, Section 5 — "With Seliose, the Daythunder Knight, striking down the Eye of Twilight, the divine war between Nikador and Aquila, which spanned centuries, came to an end..." "Thereafter... the Skyfolk gradually declined, numerous sky fortresses were abandoned, and their descendants began to establish themselves on the land..." "Finally, those descended from Seliose the Daythunder Knight were welcomed by the scholars under Cerces and established the Twilight Courtyard in the Grove..." Hmm, the historical records seem slightly at odds with our memories... but the details from that time have become somewhat hazy to us.

**Hyacine:** Oh, I forgot... For Lady Tribbie, history is more than just words on a page — it's something you've personally lived through.

**Little Ica:** Doot! Doot-Doot, Doot!

**Hyacine:** Heh, Little Ica loves listening to the stories from the legends. I'm sure you're also imagining, one day, you'll be as awe-inspiring as the winged beasts by Seliose's side? That day will come! I believe it! Just keep growing... ...Ah, Lord Krateros?

**Krateros:** Miss Hyacine... ...And Lady Tribbie, good to see you're doing alright. I heard from the citizens you were nearby and came to check.

**Tribbie:** Doing... alright? What's going on, Krateros?

**Krateros:** There's been another violent incident at the market. A shop was smashed and looted. It's the third time this week.

**Hyacine:** How can this be? I always thought that with Lady Aglaea here, Okhema would remain peaceful...

**Krateros:** That was true in the past. But these days... forgive my bluntness, her senses may not be as sharp as they once were.

**Tribbie:** Agy...

**Krateros:** In my opinion, malevolent forces have noticed the slack in the golden threads and are now plotting to exploit the opportunity.

**Tribbie:** The citizens' assembly just wrapped up... Why is there still unrest?

**Krateros:** The Chrysos Heirs won by only one vote, Lady Tribbie. From my years of experience, a narrow victory often walks hand-in-hand with unrest.

**Clementine:** ...Trouble! Miss Hyacine... trouble!

**Hyacine:** ...Clementine? Take a breath... What's going on?

**Clementine:** Please! Something terrible has happened at Marmoreal Palace!

**Krateros:** What is it, doctor? Calm yourself and talk us through!

**Clementine:** Lady Aglaea...

**Tribbie:** ...!

**Clementine:** It's... it's Lady Aglaea who's in trouble!

---

### Part 97 — `databank/missions/chapter-04-fall-at-dawn.md`
**Context:** Chapter 4 — The Fall at Dawn's Rise (v3.3) > Mission 5 — Grove, Judge the Past and Present > The Vortex of Genesis — Meeting Cipher and Tribbie
**Kind:** collective-pointer
**Id:** `chapter-04-fall-at-dawn__the-vortex-of-genesis-meeting-cipher-and-tribbie`
**Collective:** `databank/memories/collective/chapter-04-fall-at-dawn.md`
**Witness:** spoke

*(Dan Heng uses Phagousa's spirit water art to open a path to the Vortex of Genesis. Hyacine encounters Tribbie and Cipher there.)*

**Tribbie:** ...Do you have to go already, Ciphy? It's been ages since you were last in Okhema.

**Cipher:** That's alright, big sister Tribbie! I've gotten used to life on the road. Besides, your prized student... gave me an incredibly tough mission to handle.

**Tribbie:** Cut Snowy some slack, Ciphy. He's still so young, yet he's shouldering all the responsibilities Agy left behind... Besides, Snowy's not the kind to ask for help lightly. Since he came to you, it means you're the best person for the job.

**Cipher:** You really spoil him, big sis Tribbie. ...Just kidding, I get it. That Deliverer boy does have a certain charisma about him. It reminds me of Aglaea in her prime.

**Hyacine:** Lady Tribbie, and... this graceful lady must be Miss Cipher. I'm Hyacinthia from the Twilight Courtyard. Everyone calls me Hyacine — pleased to meet you!

**Cipher:** Tsk, bright and cheery as a lamp... ...Ahem. Well, hello there, pink child. I'm Cifera from the long-fallen city-state of Dolos. So you're from the Twilight Courtyard? Does that mean...

**Hyacine:** Yes, I'm a descendant of the Sky people, the Chrysos Heir about to take over the authority of Sky.

**Cipher:** Well, that's... unexpected. I figured Seliose's descendant would be... I don't know. Taller? Stronger?

**Tribbie:** Don't judge a book by its cover, Ciphy. Cinny is a master of her craft. She's the most skilled and dependable physician in all of Amphoreus.

**Cipher:** Ahaha, all this talk about missions... you're making it sound way too serious. You've gotta look out for yourself, girl! Don't push yourself too hard. I'd hate to see that cute face all scratched up by Aquila's claws... Anyway, I've got a mission to handle, so I'll get going. Good luck, Miss Hyacine. And if things get tough, just let that Deliverer take the lead!

---

### Part 101 — `databank/missions/chapter-05-sun-set-to-die.md`

**Kind:** collective-pointer

*(Approach Mydei, Trinnon, and Phainon)*

**Mydei:** ...Is this your idea of an honorable duel?

**Trinnon:** Bloodshed isn't the way. We and Agy don't want to see Chrysos Heirs turn on each other.

**Trinnon:** True warriors know when to sheathe their blades. The impartial Talanton shall oversee this duel. You need only place your offerings upon their scales...

**Trinnon:** ...Something that has more weight than the fate of the world.

**Phainon:** Which means only those who understand the world's weight are worthy to carry it... Is this how you plan to test our convictions?

**Phainon:** Well, Kremnoan, looks like you won't be able to punch your way through this one.

**Mydei:** It makes no difference. One last chance, new recruit. Walk away, and I'll spare your life.

**Mydei:** Otherwise — come and face me! I'll give you a warrior's death!

**Phainon:** ...

**(Trailblazer):** (Hard to imagine they once clashed like this...)

**Phainon:** Holy Maiden, I can't help but feel like a pawn that has stumbled into some greater political game.

**Trinnon:** Every hero was once a child. And every child must grow.

**Phainon:** That so? I don't even know why I'm standing here right now.

**Phainon:** Look at me, just a common soldier. Since swordsmanship won't decide the winner, Lady Goldweaver should be here instead. My devotion to the holy city could never compare to hers.

**Trinnon:** What if we told you this duel isn't about winning or losing, but rather in the choice you make?

**Phainon:** Are you saying that Lady Goldweaver is testing me?

**Trinnon:** Every Chrysos Heir is indispensable to the Flame-Chase Journey. And you and the crown prince of Kremnos are both a part of the prophecy.

**Trinnon:** What the duel marks is not an ending, but a beginning. So, listen to your heart. We wish only to hear your answer.

**Phainon:** ...

**Phainon:** True enough... The voice within has never failed me. It doesn't matter what the result is... I'll give it everything I have.

**Trinnon:** Spoken like a Chrysos Heir. Now go, Phainon, your journey has only just begun.

**Phainon:** That's right, comrade! It's time for us to teach that proud prince a lesson together!

---

### Part 103 — `databank/missions/chapter-05-sun-set-to-die.md`
**Context:** Chapter 5 — For the Sun is Set to Die (v3.4) > Mission 3 — (Side) Hero, Return to That Peace of Home > Accept the Test of Talanton > Talk to Phainon, optional
**Kind:** collective-pointer
**Id:** `chapter-05-sun-set-to-die__accept-the-test-of-talanton-2`
**Collective:** `databank/memories/collective/chapter-05-sun-set-to-die.md`
**Witness:** spoke

**Phainon:** But here and now, my heart says something else...

**Phainon:** This card... It's everything I have left. Nothing weighs heavier.

**???: ** Perhaps that itself is the answer.

**Trinnon:** Look, Phainon...

**Cyrene:** It's so nice to see you all again! Lady Trinnon, Phainon, and...

**Cyrene:** Hello! Seeing you here sure is a sweet surprise♪

> *(Trailblazer)* Cyrene? But how...
> *(Trailblazer)* Did I stumble into the wrong Amphoreus?

**Cyrene:** Why so surprised? Could it be... did you miss me?

**Phainon:** Cyrene?

**Phainon:** This is... incredible. I was literally just thinking about how you might be here, but I never imagined I'd see you this soon.

**Cyrene:** Hehe, I was supposed to leave with the priests, but then I heard Lady Maiden was with a white-haired young man...

**Cyrene:** I figured it had to be you, so I snuck back here!

**Phainon:** Ha... That's Cyrene for you. Unfortunately, this isn't a good time for reminiscing. That Kremnoan over there...

**Mydei:** Still playing house, boy?

**Phainon:** ...You get the idea.

**Cyrene:** Quite the hothead. Y'know, you two just might get along.

**Cyrene:** Don't worry. You already know what the answer is, don't you?

**Cyrene:** If that's your choice, I am with you.

**Cyrene:** I am with you because I remember it all too. The fields of Aedes Elysiae, those red leaves, that little swing of mine beneath the tree where I'd dream the sweetest dreams. And those dear fairies in the Membrance Maze... It hurts so much knowing they're gone forever...

**Cyrene:** The pain speaks for itself. Our hometown may have been just a tiny village, but to us, it was the whole world.

**Cyrene:** But... that's not enough, Phainon. Not nearly enough.

**Cyrene:** Even though hatred can help people grow, and turn a boy into an unbreakable warrior...

**Cyrene:** What you need now is victory. You can't just stack the scales with your past suffering...

**Cyrene:** You have to throw the weight of your "future" onto them as well.

**Phainon:** The future...? What do you mean?

**Cyrene:** Maybe that was a bit too cryptic for you, but it's actually really simple!

**Cyrene:** Why not have our mutual friend here stand on the scales with the "Deliverer"?

**Cyrene:** How about it? Can you help us?

> *(Trailblazer)* Me...?
> **Cyrene:** Of course, after all... you're what we aspire to.
> *(Trailblazer)* Wouldn't that be cheating?
> **Cyrene:** Hehe, relax, our dear crown prince won't have a clue~

**Phainon:** ...Let's try that.

**Phainon:** The fate of Okhema hangs on this duel. I must claim victory, for their sake.

**Phainon:** I will stake everything I have. Stand with me, comrade, and we shall become heroes.

> *(Trailblazer)* Remember the past, and become the hero of tomorrow.

**Phainon:** Let's. Together we'll prove that what weighs more than the fate of the world...

**Phainon:** ...Is the resolve to shoulder an immutable past, and carry it into the future.

---

**Trinnon:** Talanton, the fair, the just, they of unassailable integrity — the challengers have made their choices.

**Trinnon:** In the name of the Holy Maiden of the Three Fates, lower your scales, weigh destiny, and reveal unto us your verdict!

**Mydei:** Your offering is... a card?

**Phainon:** Yes, a flimsy little card. That is my choice. Just know that it's called the "Deliverer."

**Mydei:** Interesting. Very well — if you can beat me with this card, then that's what I'll call you from now on!

**Phainon:** It's a deal.

**Cyrene:** The scales... they're wavering...

**Trinnon:** Talanton is deliberating on the outcome. It appears the convictions of both sides are... equally matched.

**Mydei:** ...

**Phainon:** ...

**Phainon:** This is... suffocating. Like the whole world's weight is on my shoulders.

**Phainon:** But I believe... what my heart tells me...

**Titan's Voice:** *Titan's murmurs*

**Mydei:** Finally decided?

**Trinnon:** "Hear thee, children of humanity: I shall pronounce judgment, for the difference is now clear."

**Trinnon:** "The one whose offering weighs more than the fate of the world is—"

**Phainon:** ...

**Mydei:** ...

**Trinnon:** "One from Aedes Elysiae, the Nameless Hero."

**Phainon:** ...

**Cyrene:** ...♪

**Titan's Voice:** *Titan's murmurs*

**Trinnon:** "However, heed this..."

**Trinnon:** "The true weight of conviction originates not from itself..."

**Trinnon:** "...but rather in that which tips the scales, hope itself."

**Mydei:** What's that supposed to mean?

**Cyrene:** I think the Titan means that it's not anyone's personal convictions that outweigh the fate of the world.

**Cyrene:** It's about a wish, one which we all share — you, me, everyone here. What tipped the scales wasn't Phainon's conviction alone, but the collective hopes carried by this oracle card — humanity's desperate call for a Deliverer.

**Phainon:** Yes, even you and your people, Kremnoan.

**Mydei:** Placing us both on the same side of the scales? Foolish. We Kremnoans are your mortal enemies. Such weak idealism will only get you skewered on our spears.

**Phainon:** Fine by me. I've never claimed to be the Deliverer from the card anyway.

**Phainon:** I'm a soldier, and if it's my fate to bleed my last on the battlefield, then so be it. I'll fight to my last breath. And on this, I know that we're the same...

**Phainon:** That's all a "hero" really is. Someone with that kind of conviction. And the "Deliverer"? Just the sum of all those heroes.

**Aglaea:** A brilliant explanation.

**Aglaea:** Just as prophesied: "On this day, the final two heroes shall join the Flame-Chase Journey."

**Mydei:** I should have known from the start. They all say Lady Goldweaver is quite brazen, but you're the first to even imagine that prophecies could persuade a Kremnos warrior.

**Aglaea:** Oh, I have a thousand ways to tame a lion. But this lion didn't come here for bloodshed, did he?

**Hyacine:** The Kremnoans far outmatch Okhema's forces, yet we haven't lost a single soul. That's no accident, is it?

**Mydei:** Do you honestly think Kremnoans, renowned for our love of battle, would show mercy?

**Trinnon:** Your forces are equally renowned for their iron discipline.

**Trinnon:** And so, we can only assume that this is on your orders.

**Mydei:** Perhaps Okhema isn't as hidebound as the rumors say. You grasp the true marrow of power more clearly than those Elders.

---

### Part 110 — `databank/missions/chapter-06-before-their-deaths.md`
**Context:** Chapter 6 — Before Their Deaths (v3.5) > Sub-Mission 2: Sun, Repel Stars and Pale Moon > Speak with Aglaea and Tribbie > Talk to Tribbie and Aglaea
**Kind:** collective-pointer
**Id:** `chapter-06-before-their-deaths__speak-with-aglaea-and-tribbie-6`
**Collective:** `databank/memories/collective/chapter-06-before-their-deaths.md`
**Witness:** spoke

*(Talk to Tribbie and Aglaea)*
**Tribbie:** Agy, you're still so listless today...
**Tribbie:** Did you have trouble sleeping again?
**Aglaea:** ...Yes.
**Tribbie:** We know. The Titans' prophecy haunts you... If you want, you can talk to us, just like when you were young.
**Tribbie:** No matter what, we'll always remain by your side.
**Aglaea:** I know. I also understand that fate cannot be changed. It is not the future that I worry about...
**Aglaea:** I spent all last night weaving clothes and linen for the refugees. The Chrysos War is born of injustice and has shattered their dignity. They deserve at least some basic decency.
**Aglaea:** If Amphoreus one day abandons this... then all of us, commoners, Chrysos Heirs, Titans, become no better than beasts.
**Aglaea:** Oh? We have guests. Welcome. I am Aglaea, the Goldweaver.
**Aglaea:** ...These rags on you are not worthy of the title of "Deliverer." I must prepare some proper attire for you.

> *(Trailblazer)* It's been a while, Aglaea.
> **Aglaea:** What is the matter? Your gaze seems somewhat unsure.
> *(Trailblazer)* Hello. Nice to meet you for the first time.
> **Aglaea:** First time? That's not what I gather from your eyes.
> **Aglaea:** Does your "Aglaea" differ so much from me?
> **Tribbie:** ...Greetings, Deliverer from beyond the sky. We are Tribbie of Janusopolis. It's very nice to meet you...
> **Tribbie:** Although introductions are probably unnecessary. We know you've witnessed the future of the Flame-Chase Journey, even more clearly than we have.
> **Cyrene:** Witnessed the future of the Flame-Chase Journey? The prophecy also told you about that?
> **Tribbie:** Indeed! **Oronyx** reshaped Amphoreus through the prophecy. Although, regarding the Deliverer... there were not many details beyond your appearance and the time of your arrival.
> **Tribbie:** So, please don't worry. Feel free to relax and relax some more.
> *(Trailblazer)* Oronyx's prophecy?
> **Tribbie:** It was Oronyx who told mankind that the Deliverer from beyond the sky would descend upon Amphoreus.
> *(Trailblazer)* Wasn't it Kephale?
> **Tribbie:** Kephale issued prophecies too, but it was Oronyx who told mankind that the Deliverer from beyond the sky would descend upon Amphoreus.
> **Aglaea:** More crucially, they revealed a future entirely different from the Era Nova.
> **Aglaea:** Though we still must challenge the gods... in the Time Titan's prophecy, this path leads not to a new world but to the **true starry skies**...
> **Tribbie:** This is also what the Great Imperator has been striving for all her life. Your arrival further confirms everything.
> **Cyrene:** This... can't be real.
> **Tribbie:** Even you find this surprising? Fate... really is unpredictable.
> **Aglaea:** Perhaps the upcoming meeting will hold even more surprises.

---

**Aglaea:** No need for that. This banquet is just too loud, and nothing and no one here is interesting. I just don't really want to be here.

**Tribbie:** You take everything too seriously. At this rate, life's gonna be real tough for you later on.
**Aglaea:** You're the least qualified to lecture me about that, teacher.
**Cyrene:** ...

> *(Trailblazer)* Are you hesitating?

> **Tribbie:** The Imperator? No. We haven't.
> **Aglaea:** Our sovereign has always acted however she pleases. Though she gave us ranks, she'd never tell anyone her whereabouts.
> **Aglaea:** Perhaps the only thing she tolerates are chess pieces... and the sky reflected upon her crown.
> **Cyrene:** I see...
> **Aglaea:** Why do you two look so distressed? That's not what the Deliverer should look like.
> **Aglaea:** Why don't we have a toast, while we still have time for pleasure? Here, to the destinies and sacrifices of the next millennium.
> **Tribbie:** Then we will toast too. To the tomorrow that will set fate free!
> *(Trailblazer)* To the Flame-Chase Journey!
> *(Trailblazer)* To Tribbie and Aglaea!
> *(Trailblazer)* Hail to the New World!
> **Crowd:** Cheers!

**Kind:** collective-pointer

**Witness:** spoke

**Cyrene:** Look, the scenery of the past... has resurfaced.

*(Approach the Fragments of Recollection)*
**Aglaea Etched in Memory:** Time flies. Two hundred years have passed since the Deliverer's fall...
**Tribbie Etched in Memory:** We had to return three Coreflames of Law, Romance, and Ocean to stop Lygus. But even so...
**Tribbie Etched in Memory:** Terravox has fallen, the two geniuses vanished without a trace, and Cerydra embraced her fate... At this rate...
**Aglaea Etched in Memory:** "Era Nova" will come, and Amphoreus will fall into the Destruction.
**Tribbie Etched in Memory:** ...
**Tribbie Etched in Memory:** It seems the key to rewriting the end once again lies in the Coreflame of Worldbearing.
**Tribbie Etched in Memory:** According to the instructions left behind by those two geniuses, we must get it into (Trailblazer)'s hands, no matter the cost.
**Aglaea Etched in Memory:** But how?
**Aglaea Etched in Memory:** We need to find a way to hide the Coreflame, and we must buy enough time to keep Lygus at bay...
**Tribbie Etched in Memory:** About that... Reney might have some good news.
**Tribbie Etched in Memory:** **She had that dream again last night, but the Titan's voice was clearer than ever this time...**
**Tribbie Etched in Memory:** **She said: Pass the flame into the future.**
**Aglaea Etched in Memory:** Is that a prophecy from Oronyx? She chose to side with humanity this time and hide one of her kin's Coreflames?
**Aglaea Etched in Memory:** ...It seems Amphoreus' fate isn't sealed just yet.
**Tribbie Etched in Memory:** You're right. History has changed. Humans and Titans are both giving their all to keep this world going.
**Tribbie Etched in Memory:** Perhaps a relay across the ages is finally about to begin somewhere out there.
**Cipher Etched in Memory:** Unfortunately, life will never go smoothly. Ladon's army is already at our gates, and their commander...

---

### Part 115 — `databank/missions/chapter-06-before-their-deaths.md`
**Context:** Chapter 6 — Before Their Deaths (v3.5) > Sub-Mission 4: Zephyr, Uplift Bygone Dust Cloudsward > Go Forth. Pursue the Shadows Shrouded in Secrecy
**Kind:** collective-pointer

**Witness:** spoke

**Cipher Etched in Memory:** So what you're saying is... Princess Homebody... is never coming back, is she?

*(Approach the Fragments of Recollection)*
**Cipher Etched in Memory:** She just left... like that?
**Tribbie Etched in Memory:** Since Cas has made up her mind, we can't let her resolve be in vain.
**Cipher Etched in Memory:** But between staying in Okhema and going to Styxia... What difference does it make when it's death either way!
**Cipher Etched in Memory:** I'm against **relocating**. With Kephale's protection, isn't Okhema far safer than that dead city?
**Tribbie Etched in Memory:** Cas's plan doesn't involve any "relocation" at all.
**Cipher Etched in Memory:** ...Huh?
**Tribbie Etched in Memory:** Like you said, Okhema is humanity's refuge. We never intended to get everyone moving to Styxia...
**Tribbie Etched in Memory:** It's just on Cas, Hysilens, and me to prepare the final trump card for the Deliverer.
**Cipher Etched in Memory:** Wait. What kind of trump card needs all three of you demigods to throw yourselves away?
**Tribbie Etched in Memory:** We must lay down the path to the future so when the "Deliverer" returns, they'll have a clear road to reach the location of "Era Nova".
**Tribbie Etched in Memory:** Styxia sits where death meets the ocean. There, with the combined powers of Ocean and Passage, we can summon a current that leads to the Vortex of Genesis...
**Tribbie Etched in Memory:** And the Demigod of Death will forever guard that secret path until (Trailblazer) returns.
**Cipher Etched in Memory:** ...
**Cipher Etched in Memory:** We all know Lygus will set his sights on the Vortex of Genesis.
**Tribbie Etched in Memory:** That's exactly why we have to do this.
**Cipher Etched in Memory:** Guess I underestimated Aidonia's Maiden of War. She's got way more steel than I thought.
**Tribbie Etched in Memory:** And Cipher, we need you to head to Castrum Kremnos as soon as possible. By any means necessary, forge an alliance with Strife.
**Tribbie Etched in Memory:** The prophesied crown prince has already been born. He shall become the new defender of Okhema, protecting this land after we leave.
**Cipher Etched in Memory:** You don't need to worry about that...
**Cipher Etched in Memory:** I just came back from Castrum Kremnos, only a step ahead of the news.
**Trinnon Etched in Memory:** Tribbie, something's flying towards Okhema, and it's really fast...
**Trinnon Etched in Memory:** Eek!
**Tribbie Etched in Memory:** Wh—What is it?
**Trinnon Etched in Memory:** It's... it's a spear!
**Cipher Etched in Memory:** It's the Kremnoans. Their king's already here.

**Trianne Etched in Memory:** B—But... it's gonna hurt! A lot!
**Mydei Etched in Memory:** Pain brings endless torment, but it can never destroy me.
**Mydei Etched in Memory:** I believe that you were thinking the same thing when you stepped through the Gate of Infinity and jumped off that cliff.
**Trianne Etched in Memory:** Ugh... can't argue with that...
**Mydei Etched in Memory:** In the current situation, this is the only path Okhema has left. Just do as I say...
**Mydei Etched in Memory:** **Split my soul into five pieces, forge me an immortal vessel...**
**Mydei Etched in Memory:** **Let the blade of Strife hang forever overhead, locked in battle with their enemies to the world's bitter end!**
**Trianne Etched in Memory:** ...
**Mydei Etched in Memory:** There is no need to be sad, Lady Tribios. Haven't we already taken great strides towards that promised tomorrow?
**Mydei Etched in Memory:** In this life, my people no longer have to drown in false guidance. Kremnos has washed the blood off its fate.
**Mydei Etched in Memory:** Amphoreus no longer needs a god named Strife. So let me shatter the former laws and turn the Lance of Fury back into a weapon that protects the world.
**Trianne Etched in Memory:** We... understand.
**Mydei Etched in Memory:** By the way, can you do me another favor?

### Part 132 — `databank/missions/adventure/v30.md`
**Context:** Adventure Missions — Version 3.0 (Heroic Saga of Flame-Chase) > Whereto Shall We Go > Dialogue > Investigate the outlanders headed to the Abyss
**Kind:** personal
**Id:** `v30__investigate-the-outlanders-headed-to-the-abyss`

*(As your discussion was coming to an end, Soctires' disciple came with word: A group of wounded outlanders has hidden themselves away in the Abyss. You cannot help but think of the refugees that Tribbie and the others accepted into their land... The Abyss is no place to live.)*

*(Approach the marked location)*
**Palaten the Scholar:** Master, look! Those things...
**Soctires:** Though expected, the Mad King's Kin are admittedly resilient. Palaten, you stay in a safe place. Young friend, I'll be counting on you to watch my back.
> *(Trailblazer)* Will do.
> *(Trailblazer)* Ooooh! That line's got me all fired up!
> *(Trailblazer)* I can't guarantee our survival...
**Palaten the Scholar:** Master... you knew martial arts all along?
**Soctires:** No, I simply seized the chance to rally those refugees, so they would keep calm...
**Refugee:** Aahhh! Get away from me, monsters!
**Soctires:** Now!
*(Enter battle against Furiae Archer ×2; Furiae Warrior ×2; Furiae Troupe ×1)*
**Soctires:** You all hurry to the temple. It's safe there — follow me.

*(After the battle)*
**Wounded Outlander:** Ahem!
**Palaten the Scholar:** Master, these people... they're not doing well. Some were already injured before we got here.
**Soctires:** Indeed. And by the looks of them, they haven't gotten a proper meal for a while. Who's in charge here?
**Outlander Leader:** H—Here...
*(A woman holding a child emerges from the crowd.)*
**Outlander Leader:** Thank you all so much, especially this gray-haired hero. How... How can we ever repay you?
**Soctires:** That can wait. Why didn't you go to the holy city's designated shelter? There are sentries garrisoned there. It's much safer than here.
**Outlander Leader:** Shelter... Guards...
**Outlander Leader:** I overheard the guards before, talking about some "dromas workshop incident," "drawing lots," "resource allocation" and whatnot, so I didn't dare ask them to clarify.
**Outlander Leader:** I think the burden on the holy city is already heavy enough. Even if we beg the guards to let us in, it would only add to their troubles, and in the end, it would just lead to an unpleasant situation.
**Outlander Leader:** If that's the case, we might as well look for a nearby place to rest our feet, to see if we could fend for ourselves. But who would've thought...
**Clueless Outlander Child:** Mama, will we die?
**Outlander Leader:** No, baby... It won't happen as long as mama is around.
**Clueless Outlander Child:** But Uncle Anthony, and Sister Aisha... They really look like they're going to die.
**Clueless Outlander Child:** My tummy is hungry too... Didn't you say that we'd have something to eat once we got to the holy city? Mama, when will we get there?
**Outlander Leader:** I'm sorry, darling. I'm sorry...
*(The woman crouches and holds her child tight, tears silently streaming down the corners of her eyes.)*

*(Soctires softly clears his throat and pulls you to the side.)*
**Soctires:** Anyone who just suffered so much hardship would find it difficult to keep their emotions in check. Best give that mother and child some space.
**Soctires:** The outlanders' situation is much worse than I imagined. If we keep turning a blind eye, they will at best survive for a week. That is... if they aren't attacked by wild beasts first.
**Palaten the Scholar:** *sigh* ...Such is the tragedy of life.
> *(Trailblazer)* How can we help them?
>> **Soctires:** It would be hard to bring them directly into the city, so we can only provide supplies for them. And I have an idea about that...
> *(Trailblazer)* We can't save everyone...
>> **Soctires:** But we can at least help those in front of us, can't we? I already have an idea...
> *(Trailblazer)* They brought this on themselves.
>> **Soctires:** Before you pass judgment, please remember that not everyone has a choice.
> *(Trailblazer)* (Remain silent)
>> **Soctires:** At least the worst of the journey is over for these outlanders. Now they just need some simple help to live on. I have an idea...
>>
>> **Soctires:** The holy city's shelter is only a half-day trek away from the city walls. There are guards there, so it'll be safer. It's just that the way these refugees are now... getting there would be difficult.
>> **Soctires:** They haven't much food to begin with, and that last attack has left them in urgent need of emergency medical supplies. We can provide them with these two for now. When their wounded are healed, we can escort them to the shelter.
>> **Palaten the Scholar:** But, teacher... All medicine is now considered wartime supplies in Okhema — distribution is under strict control. Most people won't even be able to buy any.
>> **Soctires:** I know. I will think of a way... at least to guarantee their survival.
>> **Soctires:** The good news is, food isn't strictly regulated. You can easily get enough food to feed these people for a week at the **Marmoreal Market**.
>> **Soctires:** So, I suggest we split up: Palaten and I will secure the medicine, while our young friend buys some food back, after which we will reconvene here.
>> **Palaten the Scholar:** Hey, do I really need to come along? I was thinking of doing the procurement with this gray-haired hero.
>> **Soctires:** I need a helper. Remember my instructions last month? I'm getting old, and there are many things that I need help for.
>> **Soctires:** If there comes a day when I haven't the strength to walk a step farther, you'll have to take my mantle and carry on guiding the people of Okhema.
>> **Palaten the Scholar:** A—As you wish, teacher...
>> **Soctires:** Well then, before we set out, is there anything our young friend wants to say?
>> *(Trailblazer)* Leave it to me.
>>> **Soctires:** Thank you, young friend... I really appreciate you accompanying me on my errands. When this is all over, I have to thank you in earnest.
>>> **Palaten the Scholar:** Didn't you say you were going to treat her to a meal? Don't forget my share.
>> *(Trailblazer)* Don't forget to reimburse me then.
>>> **Soctires:** Haha, of course! When everything's over, just give the invoice to Palaten — he's the best at doing the accounts.
>>> **Palaten the Scholar:** Teacher, being good at the ledger isn't the same as paying for stuff... Please don't tease me!
>> *(Trailblazer)* Can't we notify the officials?
>>> **Soctires:** Lives are at stake. We've got no time to do it by the book... We'll report this to the Council of Elders when the problem is resolved.
>>> **Palaten the Scholar:** It sounds like these people's fates are relying on our success. I never thought a lowly apprentice like me would get a chance to perform such heroics... Sounds awfully exciting!
>> *(Trailblazer)* What do these people have to do with us?
>>> **Soctires:** You're asking the wrong question. We should always provide all the help we can to the weak, not just because it is moral. It is the correct action, even from a utilitarian perspective.
>>> **Soctires:** Who knows when the roles of the strong and weak will be reversed? When we will also need their help? Besides, isn't Kephale's majesty in bearing the burdens of the world for the people?
>>> **Palaten the Scholar:** I never thought a lowly apprentice like me would get the chance to carry on Kephale's will. It sounds very exciting!
>> **Palaten the Scholar:** Then, we'll meet later, gray-haired hero.

*(Outlander dialogue, optional)*
**Clueless Outlander Child:** Mama, I don't want to eat this rock-hard bread anymore. My teeth are going to fall out!

**Hyacine:** Tribbie? Ah, I'm sorry. Did we disturb you...
**Tribbie:** It's alright, Cinny. Trianne has always loved these little ones, often coming to the garden to play with them.
**Tribbie:** So, are you guys also administrators for the chimeras?

>> **Hyacine:** Grayie! You were just as surprised as I was earlier. Stop pretending!

>

> **Tribbie:** Let's see whose squad can achieve a higher ranking this season!
> **Hyacine:** Sure, since Tribbie is the one who is challenging us, we'll accept the challenge!
> **Hyacine:** Grayie, you should say something too as their administrator. We may be competing against the overpowered Tribbie, but that's no reason to lose out in spirit!

>> **Hyacine:** H—How rude! Grayie, you need to be more respectful to Tribbie. But we... yes, we won't lose!

**Tribbie:** Little Gray, you're here! **We** want to ask you — have you had any... **special dreams** lately?

>
> *(Trailblazer)* I just had the "Ena's Dream" during my last trip.
>> **Aglaea:** So, you haven't had any dreams since arriving in Okhema?
>>
>> **Tribbie:** **We** and Agy are worried that after receiving the blessing of Oronyx (Time Titan), you might have had some **prophetic dreams**.
>>
>> *(Trailblazer)* Prophetic dreams? What is a "prophetic dream"?
>>
>> **Aglaea:** For example, in Okhema's history, there was a general of the alliance who once had a dream: In the dream, an eagle swooped down, and caught a doe and her fawn, but was attacked and pecked to death by crows in the end.
>> **Aglaea:** After that, he realized that dream was a **prophecy** about the war. The eagle represented the allied forces, the doe and her fawn represented the enemies and their allies, and the crows symbolized the cruelty and unpredictability of war.
>> **Tribbie:** Everything in the dream came true in reality... If you had a similar dream, Little Gray, you should pay close attention.
>> *(Trailblazer)* (Struggles to recall a recent dream)
>>
>> *(I can't remember anything.)*
>> **Tribbie:** Little Gray doesn't remember anything, so **we** won't worry. Agy, since everything is fine, let's talk shop, shall we?
>> *(Trailblazer)* So, the real plot unfolds now?
>> *(Trailblazer)* Speak, what's the matter?
>>
>> **Aglaea:** This is serious business. It's the Month of Joy once again — followers of the God of Banquets (Phagousa) from Mythemis are planning to hold a **Water Carnival** at the Court of Seasons.
>> **Tribbie:** It's called... **"Seal Slammers."**
>> **Tribbie:** Though that name really does have the taste of Phagousa's disciples, the reward from Agy is quite... serious!
>> **Aglaea:** By the name of the "Goldweaver" of Okhema, I've commissioned the Grand Craftsman Chartonus to create a **"Golden Seal Cup"** to be awarded to the winner of the competition —
>> **Aglaea:** This is a grand event. Perhaps our distinguished guest might be interested?
>> *(Trailblazer)* I'm interested! I want rewards!
>>> **Aglaea:** Sounds like I've asked the right person.
>> *(Trailblazer)* Lady Aglaea uses her kaching superpower again!
>>> **Aglaea:** It's a small token. Just a humble gesture from the host.
>> *(Trailblazer)* Another bunch of puzzle minigames? I'm not in the mood for that!
>>> **Aglaea:** Don't worry. This isn't a puzzle — it's a competition.
>>
>> **Aglaea:** Even though the threat of black tide still looms, we hope that the citizens of Okhema can use this competition to release the pent-up emotions they've been holding in for so long.
>> **Aglaea:** As the esteemed guest of the Chrysos Heirs, I hope you can immerse yourself into the culture of Okhema with this experience.
>> **Tribbie:** If Little Gray is interested, sign up at the baths tomorrow.
>> **Tribbie:** It's getting late. Hope you have a good dream tonight, Little Gray. **We**'ll take our leave now. See you at the venue tomorrow!

### Part 151 — `databank/missions/adventure/v32.md`

**Cipher:** Ah, they say that because Dorian, the Mountain Dweller, was replaced by the Spirithief, the dromas is upset and has come to protest!
**Apophis:** It chose the off-season to make its appearance, which is a relatively friendly form of protest, I suppose.
**Trinnon:** If **we**'re not mistaken... this is the "giant beast rests in the abyss" mentioned in the prophecy, right?
**Apophis:** What? There are new prophecies?
**Trinnon:** Indeed. Apart from "the golden trophy shatters with no champion," **we** have heard two more at the baths: "black tide muddles clear waters" and "giant beast rests in the abyss"...
> *(Trailblazer)* Let's call on the prophecy expert, Lady Trinnon!
> *(Trailblazer)* So, is this prophecy real?
>
> **Trinnon:** The prophecy said "black tide muddles clear waters," and the bath had been tainted with black bath salts.
> **Trinnon:** It mentioned "giant beast rests in the abyss," and here the dromas stands before us.
> **Apophis:** This... this prophecy feels too real!
> **Trinnon:** That's because the prophecy has stirred the people's hearts, causing things to incline toward "the prophecy fulfilling itself before everyone's eyes."
> **Trinnon:** In Amphoreus's history, there were plenty of things that could cause "real conflicts," regardless if the prophecies were true or not.
> **Apophis:** I understand now. As long as the prophecy is spread far and wide, someone out there is bound to fulfill it, making it come true...?
> **Apophis:** What should we do? The two previous prophecies have already come true, there's only the last one left — will the Gold Seal Cup really be broken?
> **Cipher:** With our two demigods, the seamstress and big sis around, what are you afraid of?
> **Trinnon:** Don't worry, everyone. In the name of the demigods, **we** assure you that these "three calamities" are merely fabrications.
> **Trinnon:** However, **we** cannot foresee whether any Okhema citizens might act on these prophecies. Besides, what we've heard may only be part of the whole prophecy.
> ***(Trailblazer):*** (In Phagousa's prophetic dream, there was also "look for your companions," and...)

**Phainon:** The Kremnoan dictionary may be pretty sparse, but their recipe book? Vast and diverse.
**Mydei:** Want my advice? Forget that fancy salad. The mark of a great chef is making the most of what's at hand. They don't nitpick ingredients.

*(Inquire Tribbie, Anaxa, and Hyacine)*
**Tribbie:** No, no! **We** are the seniors here. At a time like this, **we** should be asking the questions!
**Tribbie:** Everyone, pay attention! The teacher's question is: With our field trip to Membrance Maze soon coming to an end, what valuable insights or unforgettable memories have you gained?
**Trianne:** Alright, answer time! If no one raises their hand, then **we** are going to start picking people!
**Hyacine:** Ooh, me, me! Teacher Tribbie, I want to share first!
**Tribbie:** Great, Cinny! You can go first and show everyone how it's done!
**Hyacine:** Everyone, listen up. I'm going to reveal... a very important secret about Professor Anaxa!
**Anaxa:** ...
**Hyacine:** As everyone knows, the professor is extremely strict and almost never praises any student other than dromases. But!
**Hyacine:** I happened to overhear! The professor quietly asked the chief about the fairies' education methods, and went on to praise those farmers in the fields, calling them hardworking, disciplined, and even better than the Sages of the Grove! His exact words were...
**Anaxa:** "The life's work of the Grove's scholars doesn't even compare to a single seed casually sown by a fairy."
**Anaxa:** And what indeed is the problem with this? In terms of purity of heart, none can match these fairies. Their vision, untainted by Titan dogma, perceives things far beyond our comprehension.
**Hyacine:** So... the reason you asked the chief to send more fairies to volunteer at the Grove was because of this?
**Anaxa:** ...
**Anaxa:** You could say that.
**Tribbie:** No taking advantage of the fairies! Make sure they're properly rewarded! Alright, that's Cinny's turn. Who's up next?
**Trinnon:** No hands up? Then **we** are going to start calling out names!
**Trianne:** ...Naxy!
**Anaxa:** All this repeated questioning is making things rather exhausting.

**Cerydra:** I reviewed yesterday's business data and customer feedback. There are a few issues we need to address.
**Cerydra:** First, with the increase in customers, we clearly don't have enough servers. The wait time for food has gotten way too long.
**Tribbie:** That's easy! Tons of little fairies want to work here. **We** can always recruit extra hands.
**Cerydra:** Second, we need more tables and chairs for the dining area, but there's no one in charge of that role right now.
**Cyrene:** That's true. Why not appoint a "lobby manager" right now, manager?
> *(Trailblazer)* Would you like to take the job, Cyrene?
>> **Cyrene:** Aww, (Trailblazer), I'm flattered you trust me! But I've already got so much on my plate — recruiting, team management, business reports, chatting with the fairies, calling closing time...
>> **Cyrene:** Nuh-uh. I'd just slow everyone down.

>> **Phainon:** I think I can manage. With Mydei sharing some of the kitchen duties, I'll have more time to help out front.
>> **Phainon:** Plus, I know the fairies well. It'll be easy to get help making more furniture.

>> **Tribbie:** Even with three of **us**, moving tables is little tough. Maybe you can check with the rest of the crew?

>
> ***(Trailblazer):*** Then it's settled. Phainon will take over as lobby manager.
> **Cerydra:** Got it. I'll update the records, and the follow-up will be in your capable hands. If there's nothing else, this meeting is adjourned.

> *(Trailblazer)* Well, it's part of my job.

**(After closing business on Day 4)**

**Garmentmaker:** Today's Summary: Business performance remains strong, with continued growth in customer satisfaction. No negative reviews were reported.
**Garmentmaker:** The menu has received a lot of attention. This probably has something to do with the addition of the new chef.

**(At the start of Day 6)**
**Cyrene:** Everyone! Before we open for the day, there's something I'd like to discuss.
**Cyrene:** Our restaurant's starting to make a name for itself, and day-to-day operations are running smoothly. I think it's time we try something more fun.
**Tribbie:** **We**'re in! But what do you mean by "fun"?
**Cyrene:** You know how shops in the city-state host seasonal celebrations? Like Phagousa's Month of Carnival, or the anniversary of the Marmoreal Palace?
**Cyrene:** Perhaps our restaurant could adopt a similar approach, regularly... refreshing the menu with enticing new dishes? Or even revamping the decor? The key is to keep things fresh and exciting.
> *(Trailblazer)* Ah! You're talking about themed events.

> **Cyrene:** Everyone can pitch their own ideas for themes, and we'll decide by vote. How does that sound?
> **Cyrene:** (Trailblazer) will have final say. Whoever suggests the chosen theme will take the lead for that event. We'll rotate themes regularly, so it won't be too much work.
> **Cyrene:** The floor's open! Feel free to speak up~
> **Phainon:** ...
> **Phainon:** That said... I'm kind of drawing a blank.
> **Tribbie:** ...It is hard indeed.
> **Cerydra:** Heh. Since no one's willing to step up, allow me to take the reins.
> **Cerydra:** In honor and commemoration, I — leader of the Okhema Alliance, Imperator of the holy city — hereby propose the first-ever restaurant event.
> **Cerydra:** **The event theme shall be: "Imperator"!**
> **Cerydra:** During the event, all dishes must be prefixed with the noble title "Imperator," all staff shall refer to themselves as loyal subjects, and all customers must recite praises of the Imperator's glory before every meal.
> **Cerydra:** Thus, that earlier salad dish shall henceforth be renamed—
> **Cerydra:** **"Cery Salad."**
> *(Trailblazer)* ...That's a bit of a dry joke.
> *(Trailblazer)* You do have a knack for cracking people up.
> **Phainon:** I, Phainon of Aedes Elysiae, reject this proposal with full conviction.
> **Tribbie:** **We** reject it too!
> **Cyrene:** Whew... I was really worried for a second that theme might actually pass.
> **Cerydra:** ...
> **Cerydra:** Hahahahaha! Well, at least that's one way to shake things up.
> **Cerydra:** Now that the spark of creativity has been lit, I'll leave you to your brainstorming.
>
> **Phainon:** I have a proposal. Since we're a restaurant in the Maze, how about a theme inspired by Aedes Elysiae itself?
> **Phainon:** When I think of Aedes Elysiae, the golden wheat fields come to mind. During the event, we could serve wheat-based dishes and decorate the place with bundles of wheat, symbolizing a bountiful harvest.
> **Cyrene:** But if the theme is "Aedes Elysiae," wouldn't that be a bit too difficult for the little fairies to understand?
> **Tribbie:** **We** have an idea! For the first event, it has to be something super appealing!

> **Cyrene:** Alright! Both proposals are on the table. Time to vote! One vote per person, so think carefully.
> **Cyrene:** Anyone in favor of Chef Phainon's proposal, raise your hand!
> **Phainon:** ...Me.
> *(Trailblazer)* (Raises hand)
>> **Cyrene:** That makes two votes for Phainon — his and (Trailblazer)'s.
> *(Trailblazer)* (Don't raise your hand)
>> **Cyrene:** Oh dear, only Phainon voted for himself.
>
> **Cyrene:** Now, those in favor of Lady Tribbie's proposal, raise your hand!
> **Tribbie:** **We** vote for it!
> *(Trailblazer)* (Raises hand)
> *(If the player agreed with Phainon earlier)* **Cyrene:** Manager... we said one vote per person. No abusing your power.
> *(If the player didn't agree with Phainon earlier)* **Cyrene:** So that's Tribbie, (Trailblazer), and me... three votes in total.
> *(Trailblazer)* (Don't raise your hand)
> *(If the player agreed with Phainon earlier)* **Cyrene:** Alright, guess it's just Tribbie and me — two votes in total.
> *(If the player didn't agree with Phainon earlier)* **Cyrene:** Fair enough. Abstaining counts too. So that's two votes for Tribbie's proposal.
>
> **Cyrene:** Which means the final tally is—
> **Tribbie:** Uh-uh, you miscounted. **We** (Tribbie, Trianne and Trinnon) all raised our hands! That's three votes! A landslide victory!
> **Phainon:** Huh!?
> **Phainon:** ...I yield. No one can win over Tribbie when it comes to voting.
> **Cyrene:** Then it's settled! The restaurant's first themed event will be "Sweetie Festival," and the event leads will be Tribbie, Trianne, and Trinnon.
> **Cyrene:** Manager, do you approve of this arrangement?
> *(Trailblazer)* Fine by me.
> *(Trailblazer)* You'll be held responsible if it doesn't go well, you know?
> **Tribbie:** Don't worry. **We** won't let you down!
>
> **Tribbie:** ...Rock, paper, scissors!
> **Trianne:** I picked rock!
> **Trinnon:** I picked scissors.
> **Tribbie:** I picked paper~
> **Tribbie:** Oh no, another tie. **We** just can't convince each other!
> *(Trailblazer)* Can't you just settle it with heads or tails?
> *(Trailblazer)* Let me join in and give it a try.
> **Tribbie:** Little Gray, perfect timing! **We** were just hoping to hear your take!
> **Tribbie:** The restaurant's event theme is "Sweetie Festival." But here's the question: What exactly is "sweet"?
> **Trianne:** It's super simple! Anything with tons of sugar counts as sweet!
> **Trianne:** So we just need to pour a mountain of sugar into the dough!
> **Trinnon:** Trinnon disagrees. Sweetness is a more complicated feeling. If someone's in a bad mood, even the sweetest food might taste dull.
> **Trinnon:** To really create that sweetie vibe, the overall dining experience has to be spot on.
> **Tribbie:** Mmm... the little cookies Mom brought home really were yummy. Tribbie came up with Sweetie Festival because **we** wanted to recreate that kind of flavor.
> **Tribbie:** But **we** still aren't sure what needs to be done...
> *(Trailblazer)* (Agree with Trianne)
>> ***(Trailblazer):*** Did you notice? "Trianne" has "tree" in her name, and she's reliable as an old oak, her hair's as red as a maple tree in autumn, and she's sweet as maple syrup!
>> **Trianne:** Little Gray, thanks for the support. But that's not a valid reason!
> *(Trailblazer)* (Agree with Trinnon)
>> ***(Trailblazer):*** You've got a point. Dishes at high-end restaurants do feel more delicious somehow.
>> **Trinnon:** Trinnon's wondering... is that because of the environment? Or is it something else?
> *(Trailblazer)* (Agree with Tribbie)
>> ***(Trailblazer):*** Memories always taste the best, but no one can taste the exact same dish twice.
>> **Tribbie:** Tribbie understands that too. But still... how do you bring back the flavor of a memory?
>
> **Tribbie:** Since **we** can't settle on a conclusion, let's take a break! Everyone, go brainstorm — **we**'ll make Sweetie Festival a success together!
> **Trinnon:** Tribbie, you're forgetting something important.
> **Trianne:** A Sweetie Festival needs sweet ingredients!
> **Cipher:** Well, well, what do we have here? Could it be you're running into a little supply issue?
> **Cipher:** From fruits of the bough to beasts of the field — I've got everything. Care to take a look?
> **Tribbie:** Little Ciphy, you're here too! **We** want to cook something suuuper sweet. Do you have the right ingredients?
> **Cipher:** Oh, I've got plenty. Go ahead, browse all you want. My stock's full to the brim, satisfaction guaranteed!
> **Cipher:** Of course... it'll cost you.
> *(Trailblazer)* Trying to jack up prices, are we?
>> **Cipher:** Ah, my friend, you wound me! This place is smack in the middle of nowhere. Hauling supplies here takes a lot of effort.
> *(Trailblazer)* Can't you give us a discount, for old times' sake?
>> **Cipher:** Oof, no can do. This place is smack in the middle of nowhere. Hauling supplies here takes a lot of effort.
>
> **Cipher:** Enough talk, see for yourself. But no haggling, got it?
>
> **(After replenishing inventory)**
> **Cipher:** Connections are everything in business. Consider this your welcome gift.
> **Tribbie:** Whoa, this honey's super sweet! Little Ciphy's prices might be steep, but the taste is totally worth it.
> **Cipher:** Of course, these goods cost me a fortune in treasures. All of top-notch quality, I swear! Come back anytime, my friend~
> **Cipher:** Not like you've got another shop to go to anyway~
> *(Trailblazer)* I'm telling the village chief on you!
>> **Cipher:** Hey, how does the saying go...? "Knowledge ain't free these days." You want the good stuff, you gotta pay up.
> *(Trailblazer)* Monopolies are illegal!
>> **Cipher:** Oh, if we're talking monopolies, your restaurant is just as guilty. So stop judging.
>
> **Cipher:** Anyway, the little critters say your restaurant is quite something. Got anything tasty for me to try too?
> **Tribbie:** You want food from the restaurant, you gotta pay. Even you don't get a free pass, Little Ciphy.
> **Cipher:** Aw~ Don't be so stingy, big sis Tribbie~
> **Cipher:** Hmph, fine. Even if I don't get to taste for free, I've got my own ways.
> **Cipher:** I've also sent you details of my purchase methods. Buy whatever you need, anytime. Later!
>
> **Tribbie:** Little Ciphy hasn't changed a bit. Still, now that we've got honey, we can whip up a whole slew of sweet dishes!
> **Tribbie:** Little Gray, let's open for business!

**Garmentmaker:** "Mem! I'm stuck in a weight loss plateau and I can't handle high-calorie food right now!"
**Garmentmaker:** "I need dishes that are sweet without sugar, crispy without grease, and both tasty AND good for me!"

**Anaxa:** Just respond with: "Would you like some magic bullets instead?"
> *(Trailblazer)* Chef Phainon, what do you think?

>> **Hyacine:** Research from Grove scholars shows that eating a full-calorie meal once a week actually helps with fat loss.
>> **Phainon:** Ah, the famous cheat meal approach. That does make sense.
>> **Garmentmaker:** Your friend is typing...
>> **Garmentmaker:** "Thanks for the advice. But I already cheated yesterday, the day before, AND the day before that..."
>> **Mydei:** And you think that's gonna help with your... fat loss?

**(After closing business on Day 7)**

**Phainon:** The kitchen situation isn't much better. Steaks frequently come out undercooked.
**Hyacine:** At least the farm section is running smoothly, probably because Professor Anaxa runs such a tight ship.

> *(Trailblazer)* We need better staff training

>> **Mydei:** How about training them with Kremnoan military discipline? I can help with that.
>> **Cerydra:** Or... we could simply cut the lowest performers and regularly terminate staff who fail to meet expectations.
>> ***(Trailblazer):*** ...You're the real evil boss.
> *(Trailblazer)* We need a performance review system
>> ***(Trailblazer):*** How about regular performance reviews with appropriate rewards and penalties?
>> **Cerydra:** Agreed. We could also add self-assessment and peer reviews to improve accuracy.
>> **Tribbie:** Agreed! But **our** vote needs to count as three!
>> **Phainon:** I officially object.
>> **Hyacine:** I'm all for it! I can't wait to give the Professor a big fat "Fail"!
>> **Anaxa:** ?

**(At the start of Day 8)**
**Tribbie:** Today's the big day. The judges are coming! Trianne, Trinnon, are you ready?
**Trianne:** Trianne poured an entire jar of honey into the dough!
**Trinnon:** Trinnon read a mountain of books and compiled tons of tips for making food taste even better.
> *(Trailblazer)* And what about Tribbie?
> *(Trailblazer)* Someone's slacking off, huh?
> **Trianne:** Trianne saw it. Tribbie was out playing in the fields all day!
> **Tribbie:** That's not true! Tribbie was working super hard too... Tribbie just wanted to prepare a surprise for Cas.
> **Tribbie:** Alright, Little Gray's up and about. Let's get today's business started!
