*(Talk to Tribbie)*
**Tribbie:** Little Gray, you ready to head to the food review?
> *(Trailblazer)* Let's roll!
>> **Tribbie:** Let's go! Time to find Cas and our little fairy friends!
> *(Trailblazer)* Still got a couple of things to wrap up...
>> **Tribbie:** No rush! **We**'ll wait for you right here, Little Gray!

**Announcer Fairy:** The long-awaited Memchelin Food Review is officially back —
**Announcer Fairy:** Lately, "(The Trailblazer's Hearth/Maze Grand Restaurant/Aedes Elysiae Community Canteen/Chrysos Heir Activity Center/Custom Name 2)" has been gaining tons of buzz in the Membrance Maze. With delicious food and warm, thoughtful service, they've won over the hearts of our fairy folk.
**Announcer Fairy:** The recently held Sweetie Festival was another big hit. Even I sneaked over for a bite. Mem! Words can't do it justice... It's just that good!
**Announcer Fairy:** Will they pass this latest round of judging? And what will the panel of expert reviewers have to say?
**Announcer Fairy:** Let's see what'll happen!
**Tribbie:** Little Gray, can **we** ask you a favor? Can **we** decide which dishes to submit for judging?
**Trianne:** Trianne wants to know whose dish is sweeter!
**Trinnon:** There happens to be three judges, so each of **us** only needs to prepare one dish.
> *(Trailblazer)* Alright, you've got my approval.
> *(Trailblazer)* Better bring your A-game, everyone.
> **Tribbie:** Thanks, Little Gray! Just place them on that empty table, right? **We**'ll go get everything ready right away!

**Judge Fairy:** Mem?
**Trianne:** Hey, that's not fair, picking the same dish as Trianne!
> *(Trailblazer)* Guess this is what they call telepathy.
> *(Trailblazer)* I kinda feel this is to be expected?
> **Tribbie:** **We** each had our own take on what the Sweetie Festival means.
> **Tribbie:** Even if it's all pancakes, the taste can still be totally different!
> **Castorice:** I see. To help the judges out, could each of you talk about your dish a little?
> **Trianne:** Trianne first! Sweet food's gotta be super sweet! So Trianne packed that pancake with a ton of honey.
> **Trianne:** Oh, and also, Trianne secretly asked De to learn a special technique!
> **Trianne:** Simmering it over and over till the honey turned extra rich and sweet, but not too heavy!
> **Trianne:** That sums up Trianne's idea! Sweet food should make people smile the moment they taste it!
> **Castorice:** So Trianne focused on improving the ingredients and techniques. What about the other two chefs?
> **Trinnon:** Trinnon read in a book that great restaurants explain to their guests how the food is made. It helps them enjoy it even more.
> **Trinnon:** The wheat used for these pancakes was carefully selected and cultivated by the restaurant's agricultural expert. Only the best made the cut after countless trials.
> **Trinnon:** And the head chef used a signature technique from Castrum Kremnos when kneading the dough and baking it, which makes the pancakes extra crisp and really brings out the full aroma of the honey.
> **Trinnon:** **We** wonder... does this flavor suit your palate?
> *(Trailblazer)* Look at you, really starting to sound like a pro waiter at a fancy establishment!
> *(Trailblazer)* Wow, didn't expect Trinnon to be this good at bragging...
> **Trianne:** Little Gray, Trinnon will let you in on a secret: Trinnon actually rehearsed that whole speech for days just for this!
> **Castorice:** You can tell a lot of love went into that dish. Now then, what's special about yours, Tribbie?
> **Tribbie:** You'll know the moment you taste it, Cas.
> **Castorice:** Then I won't hold back!
>
> **Castorice:** ...
> **Castorice:** Besides the crisp crust and the sweet honey, there's something else... something familiar. Wait, is this...
> **Castorice:** The scent of Antila flowers?
> **Tribbie:** Cas figured it out! Yes. Tribbie added Antila petals to the pancake.
> **Tribbie:** I feel the heart of the Sweetie Festival is making people feel happy. But happiness... means something different to everyone.
> **Tribbie:** That's why a restaurant should tailor each dish to suit the guest's tastes and preferences.
> **Tribbie:** So I made this honey pie just for you, Cas!
> **Castorice:** ...Indeed, it tastes just like in my memories. Very much a taste of Aidonia. Thank you.
> **Castorice:** There's no about that Tribbie, Trinnon, and Trianne each brought something amazing to the table. Choosing just one is not easy.
> **Castorice:** But since this dish is going to represent our restaurant, I think we've got a clear winner. What do the rest of the judges think?
> **Judge Fairy:** Mem! Mem, mem!!!
> **Castorice:** Agreed. That's what I thought.
> **Castorice:** Looks like one judge had to step out halfway. So by the rules, I'll go ahead and announce the winner of this culinary showdown—
> **??:** Hold on! I haven't voted yet!
>
> **Judge Fairy Who's Late:** Sorry! I had a sudden stomachache and had to step out for a bit. My bad, everyone. Mem!
> **Judge Fairy Who's Late:** Has everyone else already given their verdict? Then let me grab a bite. Mem!
> **Trianne:** Uh-oh, Trianne's getting real nervous!
> **Judge Fairy Who's Late:** Have you all tried your own dishes? Mem.
> **Tribbie:** Yeah. One bite.
> **Judge Fairy Who's Late:** And? What'd you think? Mem.
> **Tribbie:** It was delicious! **We** really put **our** hearts into these dishes. You won't be disappointed!
> **Castorice:** Yes. The other judge and I both gave very high marks.
> **Judge Fairy Who's Late:** That good, huh? Alright, let me have a taste too.
>
> **Judge Fairy Who's Late:** ...
> **Judge Fairy Who's Late:** — WHOA!! Pfft! Pfft!!
> **Judge Fairy Who's Late:** Who dumped that much sugar in here!? Don't you know cats can't eat sweets!?
> **Trianne:** Whoa! The little fairy just turned into a little Ciphy!
> **Cipher:** ...

**Phainon:** Haha, wouldn't that be lucky! I'd love to help out in any way I can.
**Cyrene:** Guess we can always depend on you! Now, since everyone is here, let's kick off the weekly operations meeting. Anyone want to start?
**Tribbie:** **We** found something! After the Sweetie Festival, Trianne gathered feedback from the little fairies, and there was this one comment worth noticing.
*"Just coming here to eat is sooo boring! I need emotional value! Make me happy and I'll tip more! Mem!"*
**Phainon:** The tavern in the village sometimes hosts bards who share tales from the Seaside States. Maybe we could try something like that too?
**Cerydra:** In that case, I have someone I'd like to introduce to everyone.
**Hysilens:** You're too kind, Imperator. I'm hardly a partner, just a small fish drawn to the scent of celebration.
> *(Trailblazer)* The last person finally arrives...
> *(Trailblazer)* Now that's what I call an all-star restaurant.
> **Cerydra:** Dux Gladiorum, you're the expert in banquet planning. Got any suggestions for the restaurant's layout?
> **Hysilens:** I had Dux Goldweaver show me around earlier. Lord Phainon's layout for the dining area is already top-notch. No change is needed.
> **Hysilens:** But you can't throw a real feast without the right atmosphere. A good meal needs music and drinks to match. I'll jot down everything we'll need to set the mood.
> **Hysilens:** Well then, I'll be taking my leave.
> **Cerydra:** That's just how Dux Gladiorum is. She likes her solitude. Let her be. As usual, once you've picked a new theme for the next event, be sure to fill me in.
>
> **Cyrene:** The Sweetie Festival was a big hit! Guests really loved the special dishes we rolled out.
> **Cyrene:** Let's keep the momentum going! Shall we pick a theme for our next event?
> **Phainon:** How about we center it around "pots"? Just like a warrior treasures their sword, a chef's pot is their most loyal companion.
> **Phainon:** Steamers, woks, skillets... you name it. During the event, we only serve dishes made with that specific type of cookware.
> **Mydei:** Objection. That's style over substance. How are we supposed to create a satisfying menu with a gimmick like that?
> **Mydei:** It's just like someone held back by their weapon is unfit to be called a true warrior.
> **Phainon:** Oh yeah? That's why you fight bare-handed? Or is grilling the only thing people in Castrum Kremnos know how to do?
> **Mydei:** Watch your mouth, numbskull. Don't lump us in with the barbarians.
> **Cyrene:** Enough! Save it for after the meeting. Stay on topic.
> **Cyrene:** Phew, what's with all that tension? Maybe we should just have you two co-run the kitchen. Some healthy competition might actually be good for business.
> *(Trailblazer)* Actually... I've got an idea.
> ***(Trailblazer):*** Why not make the next event a "competition"?
> **Phainon:** Oh? You want me and Mydei to go head-to-head in the kitchen? Fine by me. I'm in.
> **Phainon:** You make a decent pancake, I'll give you that. But this time, I'm cooking up something that'll blow yours out of the water.
> **Mydei:** We'll see about that. Just don't show up with another salad and call it gourmet.
> **Tribbie:** **We**'re in too! This competition will definitely be the focus of those little fairies' attention!
> **Phainon:** It's settled then. You and me, three days each in the kitchen. We'll see who's a better chef.
> **Mydei:** Sounds fair. It's been a while since you cooked, so I'll let you go first.
> **Phainon:** Heh, hope your confidence doesn't come back to bite you.
> **Cyrene:** That's a lot of excitement in the air! Then it's decided: The theme for our next event will be, as the owner suggests, Chef Showdown!
> **Tribbie:** Let's gooo! Snowy takes the first move, and De goes second!
> **Phainon:** I'll be borrowing your kitchen for a few days.

**Hungry Fairy:** Mem! Why isn't it open yet? I'm growing fur-roots here!
**Phainon:** Can't believe our soft launch is already such a hit. The crops in the field should be ready too. Now it's time to go all out, partner.
**Cyrene:** Looks like our head chef can't wait to get started. Why don't we open for business now? Let's not keep the fairies waiting.
> *(Trailblazer)* Alright, let's do it.
>
> **Cerydra:** But before we open for the day, I need to confirm. What's your vision for the restaurant's future?
> **Cerydra:** As the supervisor, I'll need to plan accordingly based on your direction.
> **Tribbie:** A plan? I don't really get that stuff... Isn't it enough if more faeries come to eat?
> **Cerydra:** If we're just talking traffic, we've got two approaches: run discounts, or boost table turnover. Each one puts different demands on the team. And more customers means more management pressure, which is not always a good thing.

### Part 170 — `databank/missions/adventure/v35-37.md`
**Context:** Adventure Missions — Versions 3.5–3.7 > Fairy Tree Hollow Dive > Dialogue > Get ready and attend the Memchelin Food Review > Talk to Cyrene
**Kind:** collective-pointer
**Id:** `v35-37__get-ready-and-attend-the-memchelin-food-review-6`
**Collective:** `databank/memories/collective/v35-37.md`
**Witness:** spoke

**Cyrene:** The food review is about to begin. Ready to hit the road?
> *(Trailblazer)* Come on, let's get going!
>> **Cyrene:** It's our first time at a review like this. I'm somewhat nervous... Wonder what kind of score we'll get?
>> **Cyrene:** Here's hoping it's a great start. Come on, let's move.
> *(Trailblazer)* Still got a couple of things to wrap up...
>> **Cyrene:** No worries. Once you're ready, just come find us.

**Announcer Fairy:** Memem... Mem! The Memchelin Food Review is about to begin. Please get ready, mem!
**Tribbie:** The fairies are really serious about this. Is it that formal of an event?
**Phainon:** Fairies hold food to the highest standards. As we all know, there's no matter too trivial in food quality.
**Announcer Fairy:** In the spirit of fairness, impartiality, and transparency, our judges will taste each dish on-site and deliver their final verdict, mem!
**Announcer Fairy:** Now then, mem! Please place your submitted dish on the designated table, mem!
**Cyrene:** We'll need something that best represents our restaurant. The choice of our signature dish now rests in your hands.
*(This is a huge moment. The food review can make or break a restaurant's reputation. This calls for a thorough deliberation. You picture the judges savoring the dish... And finally, the answer comes to your mind.)*
***(Trailblazer):*** The dish we're presenting is—
> *(Trailblazer)* Colorful Salad.
> *(Trailblazer)* Lettuce Salad.
>
> **Phainon:** Got it. I'll place the dish you chose onto the table.
> **Announcer Fairy:** Aside from the two traditional fairy judges, we've also invited a mystery guest for this review!
> **Announcer Fairy:** Word has it she's traveled far and wide, tasted countless delicacies, and is especially skilled at preparing raw ingredients. They say she's earned the title... "The Kitchen Slayer!"
> **Announcer Fairy:** What kind of twist will this special guest bring to the Memchelin Food Review? Stay tuned and find out, mem!
> **Phainon:** ...
> *(Trailblazer)* You're not getting nervous, are you?
> **Phainon:** Ha, haha... Of course not! I'm a professional chef now. A little food review's nothing! Totally under control!
> **Cyrene:** Who could this mysterious special guest be? The village chief never mentioned anything about this beforehand.
> *(Trailblazer)* I've got a feeling it's a familiar face.
> *(Trailblazer)* Shall we use the method of elimination?
> **Tribbie:** Ah! **We** see her! That new judge is—
>
> **Castorice:** Miss (Trailblazer), it's been a while. I didn't expect to meet you all here.
> **Phainon:** Miss Castorice? So, you're the special guest judge the fairies invited.
> **Tribbie:** Cas! You here to play with the little fairies too?
> **Castorice:** I'm a bit embarrassed to say it, but I actually applied to be the restaurant's chef. Unfortunately, my skills didn't pass the village chief's assessment.

### Part 173 — `databank/missions/adventure/v38.md`
**Context:** Amphoreus — Adventure Missions (v3.8) > Chrysos Awoo Championship > Dialogue > Become a chimera trainer > Talk to Hyacine
**Kind:** collective-pointer
**Id:** `v38__become-a-chimera-trainer-2`
**Collective:** `databank/memories/collective/v38.md`
**Witness:** spoke

*(Talk to Hyacine)*
*(Just another day in peaceful Okhema)*
**Hyacine:** Ah, Grayie, you're here! Are you ready for the Chimera World Tournament?
**Tribbie:** With Little Gray's abilities, they'll surely get a high ranking. Hehe, but Lady Tribbie isn't someone to be messed with either.
> *(Trailblazer)* Hold on, let me explain first...
>
> **Hyacine:** Grayie, you must be quite familiar with Little Chimera by now. In Amphoreus, there's a long-standing tradition of arranging battles for chimeras, and those who command them are known as **Chimera Trainers.**
> **Hyacine:** This world tournament is where chimera trainers compete against each other.
> **Tribbie:** Although it's a competition, no chimeras will be harmed. Don't worry, Little Gray. Everyone knows their boundaries!
>
> *(Trailblazer)* Oh, so the chimera's not getting enough work, huh?
>
> **Hyacine:** All work and no play makes even the smartest chimera dull. Grayie, why don't you give them a nice long break?
> **Tribbie:** Combat is part of their job too, Little Gray. Just think of it as team building!
> *(Trailblazer)* They have to fight to survive!
> *(Trailblazer)* Not this kind of team building...
>
> **Hyacine:** All the Little Chimeras from the Garden of Life will participate in this tournament. You'll get to see many familiar faces there, Grayie.
> *(Trailblazer)* How do I enter the tournament?
>
> **Hyacine:** Don't worry, we've already made arrangements for you, Grayie.
> **Tribbie:** Ahem, attention class! Lady Tribbie will now explain everything to you, so pay close attention!
> **Tribbie:** Well, it all started at the finals of the last "Chimera World Tournament"...
---
**Tribbie:** Back then, I faced off against little Imperator in the grand finals.
**Hyacine:** Let me help you review the battle situation!
---
*(After several grueling rounds of battle, Tribbie's chimera squad was finally defeated...)*
**Hyacine:** It was a match that had all of Okhema watching, and I was there to witness it. In the end, the Great Imperator won by a narrow margin.
**Tribbie:** Little Imperator, that's so unfair! That ability is way too overpowered! But hey, after hearing all this, Little Gray must be getting it now, right?
**Tribbie:** The Chimera World Tournament is a tournament where trainers compete against each other with their chimeras. There is a total of thirteen contestants, and with Little Gray joining as a special guest, that makes fourteen!
> *(Trailblazer)* Thirteen... that number rings a bell.
> *(Trailblazer)* So you're the prophesied chimera trainer, huh?
>
> **Hyacine:** Lady Tribbie and I will be there too. We're all your friends, Grayie, so don't be nervous. Just think of it as a game with everyone.
> **Tribbie:** Also, considering you don't have your own chimera, we discussed and decided...
> **Tribbie:** **Little Gray can use any player's lineup to compete. In other words, you have complete freedom!**
> *(Trailblazer)* Select your hero!
> *(Trailblazer)* To give freely is to show true courtesy.
>
> **Hyacine:** I'll explain the more complex rules during actual matches. Lady Tribbie, let's begin now.
> **Tribbie:** All right! Little Gray, join **us** and become a chimera trainer!
> *(Trailblazer)* Go, chimera!

---

### Part 175 — `databank/missions/adventure/v38.md`

*(Talk to Hyacine again, if the tutorial round was not completed)*
**Hyacine:** I'll explain the more complex rules during actual matches. Lady Tribbie, let's begin now.
**Tribbie:** All right! Little Gray, join **us** and become a chimera trainer!
> *(Trailblazer)* Go, chimera!
---
*(After winning the tutorial round)*
**Hyacine:** You've learned the battle rules so quickly, Grayie. You really are talented!
**Tribbie:** Hehe, Lady Tribbie did a great job too! But that was just the tutorial. The real match is about to begin.
**Mydei:** I watched your recent battle. Not bad, you're indeed a formidable opponent.
**Fig Stew:** HKS!
**Mydei:** ...
**Mydei:** I've told you many times, don't throw that word around carelessly.
> *(Trailblazer)* Just following your lead.
> *(Trailblazer)* You must've said it way too many times!
>
> **Tribbie:** This is De's personal chimera. Not only is it fierce in combat, but it's also quite accomplished in the ancient Kremnos language.
> **Hyacine:** Krenabis can also help me take care of patients, and they're such quick learners!
> **Krenabis:** Awoo! I can also help Cinny unite the team members and secure victory in the match!
>
> *(Trailblazer)* So, a personal chimera is...
>
> **Tribbie:** It's a very special chimera trained by each trainer, with a highly distinctive appearance!
> **Hyacine:** This "similarity" phenomenon still hasn't found a proper explanation. Let's just say... the Blessing of Cerces is quite mysterious, isn't it?
>
> *(Trailblazer)* Is Little Ica going to lose her job?
>
> **Hyacine:** What are you thinking, Grayie? Little Ica is a mighty winged horse, a child of the sky. There's no competition between her and Little Chimera.
> **Tribbie:** **Our** Candied Apple is super cute too! Little Gray has already seen them!
>
> *(Trailblazer)* Don't I get my own chimera?
>
> **Hyacine:** Hmm... Maybe if you make a few more wishes under the garden tree, your little chimera will fall right down from it?
> **Hyacine:** Just kidding! These little ones are Cerces's blessing. Once you spend more time bonding with them, they might just show up in front of you on their own.
>
> *(Trailblazer)* I can't wait to battle!
>
> **Mydei:** Hmph, then come challenge me fair and square.
> **Tribbie:** The contestants are almost all here, and the tournament's about to begin. The next time we meet, we'll be rivals, Little Gray!
> **Hyacine:** Let's go. The registration point is not far from here. I'll take Grayie there.

### Part 178 — `databank/wiki/lore/tribbie-dialogue.md`
**Context:** Tribbie/Dialogue > "Eternal Holy City" Okhema > Dialogue
**Kind:** personal
**Id:** `tribbie-dialogue__dialogue`

;(After completing Hero, Bear Thy Coreflame)
**Tribbie:** Ah, Little Gray! You've come to the garden to relax, too? The scenery here is beautiful. **We** often come here when feeling overwhelmed.
: I want to chat with you, Tribbie!
**Tribbie:** No problem. What would you like to discuss, Little Gray? Do you want to hear **our** song of prophecy again?
:: I can never tell you, Trianne, and Trinnon apart.
**Tribbie:** Don't worry! It happens all the time. So, **we** created a slogan to help people tell the difference. Listen up, Little Gray...
**Tribbie:** Quiet Trinnon has little to say, lively Trianne loves to play, and well-behaved Tribbie roams around all day!
**Tribbie:** What do you think? You won't get us mixed up again from now on, right?
::: I'd like to get to know a bit more about Trianne!
**Tribbie:** Trianne is the liveliest and bravest among **us**. Although she looks like a child like the rest of **us**, she always dashes headlong into danger and protect everyone as a brave warrior!
**Tribbie:** That is why Trianne actively took on the role of "gatekeeper" and uses the "Century Gate" to help people escape from danger.
**Tribbie:** I just... I hope that she doesn't overexert herself.
::: I want to know more about Trinnon!
**Tribbie:** Trinnon is the most cool-headed among **us**. When she thinks about a problem, she considers every possible angle. Everyone says that she has the makings of a scholar.
**Tribbie:** If we lived in a time of peace and prosperity, maybe Trinnon could have been a knowledgeable scholar or teacher!
::: I would like to know more about you, Tribbie!
**Tribbie:** Um... You want to hear Tribbie's comment on Tribbie, huh? Kind of an odd request but sure. Everyone says that Tribbie is the most well-behaved of **us**. But, **we** think that Trianne and Trinnon are well-behaved, too.
**Tribbie:** Sometimes people have a clearly defined idea of a person but cannot give an exact reason for it. In **our** eyes, you are an extremely reliable person, Little Gray! Though you are not Agy or Snowy, you are just as reliable as they are.
**Tribbie:** Hehe, Tribbie wonders what she's like in your eyes.
**Tribbie:** If you still cannot tell the difference, there is actually a simpler method...
**Tribbie:** Look at **our** hairstyles! You're observant so **we** don't need to explain in too much detail. **We** know you'll see the difference in no time!
:: Master Tribbie, I want to learn how to use the "Century Gate"!
**Tribbie:** Ambitious, Gray Hair! It is impossible for ordinary folks to understand how to use the "Century Gate."
**Tribbie:** ...That being said, you took one look at Oronyx's miracle and learned how to use it. That should've been equally impossible... Could Tribbie be wong and you are a genius all along?
**Tribbie:** Alright, it's decided. **We** will teach you how use the Century Gate! Let's create some miracle!
**Tribbie:** Gray Hair, please call out the "Gate of Infinity" from the botton of your heart. Feel the wavering of space and listen to the distant murmurs in your ears...
**Tribbie:** Then, cast the power that you sense out like it's a net!
::: Hya!!!
**Tribbie:** ...Gray Hair, you scared us! And it doesn't seem that the gate is opened...
::: Ha! Space bomb!
**Tribbie:** No, no! That's far too violent, Gray Hair! It sounds like you want to completely annihilate space itself!
::: I cannot feel it. Shall I use a Space Anchor?
**Tribbie:** What is that? Is it some kind of miracle of Janus that **we** do not know about?
**Tribbie:** It seems that learning to use the "Century Gate" is a bit too difficult for you now, Gray Hair. But don't be disheartened. You are already amazing! We know that you will become a great hero of Amphoreus even without the power of the "Century Gate"!
:: Your telepathy must be very convenient.
**Tribbie:** Oh, you mean the telepathy shared by Tribbie, Trianne, and Trinnon?
**Tribbie:** Yes, it is, most of the time. As long as **we** are willing, **we** can share most of our senses.
**Tribbie:** For instance, Tribbe and Trinnon and the indoorsy type, but Trianne is the opposite. WHen Trianne sees a beautiful landspace, she shares it with **us** through telepathy.
**Tribbie:** Also, when Agy needs to send people to a distant place out of the golden thread's reach, she'll ask one of **us** to join them. That way, even if teleslates won't work, Agy can reach us in time.
::: A round of applause for Okhema's greatest messengers!
**Tribbie:** Stop it, **we** are blushing. Actually, telepathy doesn't always come in handy.
::: Like when?
**Tribbie:** When **our** emotions are too intense, **we** will subconsciouly share them. For example, if one of **us** accidentally bangs our head, the others will feel the pain.
**Tribbie:** There's a funny story. Trinnon once read a sad story in the library and couldn't help but let out a few sobs.
**Tribbie:** At that moment, Trianne was out having a wonderful time in Marmoreal Market and she suddenly started sobbing too. Seeing that, everyone was worried and shocked. So they tried to stop her from crying by handing Trianne loads of tasty food.
**Tribbie:** So, if one day you see **us** suddenly shedding tears out of nowhere, no need to worry.
:: Let's talk another time. I'll see you tomorrow, Tribbie!
**Tribbie:** Okay, sure! "See you tomorrow" is such a wonderful blessing! See you tomorrow, Gray Hair!
: Let's talk another time. I'll see you tomorrow, Tribbie!
**Tribbie:** Okay, sure! "See you tomorrow" is such a wonderful blessing! See you tomorrow, Gray Hair!
;(Obtain , one-time only)
---
;(After completing Passage, Reveal the Past Once More)
**Tribbie:** Ah, Little Gray! You've come to the garden to relax, too? The scenery here is beautiful. **We** often come here when feeling overwhelmed.
:;(After beginning Chrysos Awoo Championship) 
: About the Chimera World Tournament...
**Tribbie:** Since you asked so sincerely, **we** shall tell you graciously&mdash;
**Tribbie:** Actually, the purpose of holding this Chimera World Tournament is to protect the world from devastation!
:: What? That's actually a thing?
**Tribbie:** Heh-heh, bet you didn't know that, huh? If we don't hold competitions regularly to keep the chimeras busy, they'll have to go back to work. After all, there's as much work to do as there are books in the repository!
**Tribbie:** If the chimeras work too long, their pent-up rage will sweep through all of Amphoreus. That's terrifying.
**Tribbie:** So Little Gray, you should come to compete more often when you're free! This is also to protect the world!
:: ...You're just making this up.
**Tribbie:** Ah, don't call **us** out like that! Little Gray has no sense of humor! At times like this, you should play along with **us** in telling the story!
**Tribbie:** All right, let's wrap up the chit-chat. We'll be meeting on the field soon anyway.
: I want to chat with you, Tribbie!
**Tribbie:** No problem. What would you like to discuss, Little Gray? Do you want to hear **our** song of prophecy again?
:: I want to... hear stories of your travels.
**Tribbie:** Of course! **We**'ve traveled the world for thousands of years &mdash; each of us has countless rich and fascinating experiences. What kind of story would you like to hear, Little Gray?
::: I want to hear about Tribbie's experiences.
**Tribbie:** **We**? **We** remember that when **we** left Janusopolis, **we** still held the spindle of our homeland in our hands. 
**Tribbie:** Back then, *we* were so naive &mdash; thinking that as demigods, *we* could use the spindle to spin threads of destiny and tie our friends together.
**Tribbie:** But that spindle was lost in war. Our journey wasn't smooth either. Not a single Chrysos Heir was willing to heed **our** call. Worse still, there were villains who imprisoned **us** in a cell...
**Tribbie:** Until Cerydra found **us**. It was she who united the Chrysos Heirs and turned the prophecy into law.
**Tribbie:** I wish I could introduce her to you, but alas... that opportunity is long gone.
::: I want to hear about Trinnon's experiences.
**Tribbie:** Trinnon is quiet and intellectual. Long ago, when **we** left Janusopolis together, she carried a small stone tablet, hoping to convey her knowledge and ideals to all the Chrysos Heirs who answered the call.
**Tribbie:** At first, she encountered a group of kind-hearted bards who protected her as they traveled across many lands.
**Tribbie:** However, mortal lives are fragile, and a hundred years later, she was alone again. By that time, she spoke even less than before...
::: I want to hear about Trianne...
**Tribbie:** ...Little Gray, there's no need to hesitate. **We**re no longer that sad. **We**ve already held a farewell ceremony for Trianne. If **we** don't turn our toes toward the future, Trianne will feel sad &mdash; and even angry.
**Tribbie:** Back to the topic. Trianne was the bravest among **us**. When she first set out, she held a small wooden sword, huffing and puffing as she dreamed of taking down a Titan herself!
**Tribbie:** Sadly, it didn't go so well. She was chased by several Titankins all the way from Ladon to Epos in a thrilling escape...
**Tribbie:** But Trianne proudly told **us** that it was precisely because of those experiences &mdash; being chased by the Kins and repeatedly forced to use "Century Gate" &mdash; that she became the most skilled gatekeeper among **us**!
:: Master Tribbie, I want to learn how to use the "Century Gate"!
**Tribbie:** Ambitious, Gray Hair! It is impossible for ordinary folks to understand how to use the "Century Gate."
**Tribbie:** ...That being said, you took one look at Oronyx's miracle and learned how to use it. That should've been equally impossible... Could Tribbie be wong and you are a genius all along?
**Tribbie:** Alright, it's decided. **We** will teach you how use the Century Gate! Let's create some miracle!
**Tribbie:** Gray Hair, please call out the "Gate of Infinity" from the botton of your heart. Feel the wavering of space and listen to the distant murmurs in your ears...
**Tribbie:** Then, cast the power that you sense out like it's a net!
::: Hya!!!
**Tribbie:** ...Gray Hair, you scared us! And it doesn't seem that the gate is opened...
::: Ha! Space bomb!
**Tribbie:** No, no! That's far too violent, Gray Hair! It sounds like you want to completely annihilate space itself!
::: I cannot feel it. Shall I use a Space Anchor?
**Tribbie:** What is that? Is it some kind of miracle of Janus that **we** do not know about?
**Tribbie:** It seems that learning to use the "Century Gate" is a bit too difficult for you now, Gray Hair. But don't be disheartened. You are already amazing! We know that you will become a great hero of Amphoreus even without the power of the "Century Gate"!
:: About your past...
**Tribbie:** Little Gray, you've traveled with Trinnon to the past Janusopolis and seen **our** past, haven't you?
**Tribbie:** Whatever Trinnon has seen and heard, **we** also sense. Those dusty memories, buried beneath the weight of time, have been polished by Oronyx and now shine brightly once more.
::: About your mother...
**Tribbie:** **Our** mother... Memories of her are **our** most treasures, yet also the easiest to lose. Those days she spent with **us** were so beautiful that **we** hardly dared to immerse ourselves in them.
**Tribbie:** But memories left untouched for too long become blurred when one finally tries to recall them. They return only fleetingly on rare nights, like a gentle kiss of reunion in **our** dreams...
**Tribbie:** For instance, someone once asked **us** about the five-petal flower **we** wear. **We** racked our brains, but no matter how hard **we** tried, **we** just couldn't remember its meaning.
**Tribbie:** Thanks to you, Little Gray, **we** finally realized that this five-petal flower is deeply connected to **our** mother...
**Tribbie:** Ah, Little Gray, you might not know &mdash; **our** mother's name is Mortis, and this flower **we** wear is named after her. In the legends of Janusopolis, this flower symbolizes longing, gratitude, and love that transcends time and space.
::: About Tribios...
**Tribbie:** "The one with three lives"... Time's erosion meant **we** have almost forgotten this original name
**Tribbie:** Yet, after crossing countless gates and enduring splits and fractures, **we** are no longer the Tribios **we** once were...
**Tribbie:** A thousand years later, as if fate quietly decreed it, there are only three Chrysos Heirs continuing the Flame-Chase Journey and bearing the divinity of Janus.
**Tribbie:** They are Tribbie, Trinnon, and Trianne who wait silently on the other shore.
::: About what happened to Janusopolis afterward...
**Tribbie:** After **we** left Janusopolis, the darkness that shrouded the city grew heavier. The Holy Maidens who succeeded **us** endured even stricter control &mdash; such as the current "Oracle Dreammother", who resides in Okhema.
**Tribbie:** **We** don't know if the suffering of **our** homeland was **our** fault or if it was a calamity brewing in secret for a long time. Nor do **we** know what kind of emotions we should harbor toward our homeland...
**Tribbie:** But... many years later, when **we** heard of Janusopolis' final downfall and how it twisted into the dim abyss it is today, **we** couldn't help but revisit the place several times.
::: Let's not dwell on it for now.
**Tribbie:** Mm, Little Gray, you're right... Looking back on the past every now and then is meant to draw strength from those one holds dear. But if one indulge too much in the past... one will lose sight of the path ahead.
:: Let's talk another time. I'll see you tomorrow, Tribbie!
**Tribbie:** Okay, sure! "See you tomorrow" is such a wonderful blessing! See you tomorrow, Gray Hair!
: Let's talk another time. I'll see you tomorrow, Tribbie!
**Tribbie:** Okay, sure! "See you tomorrow" is such a wonderful blessing! See you tomorrow, Gray Hair!

### Part 180 — `databank/wiki/lore/tribbie-dialogue.md`
**Context:** Tribbie/Dialogue > Beyond Time: An Eternal Page > Dialogue
**Kind:** personal
**Id:** `tribbie-dialogue__dialogue-2`

;(Slumbering Courtyard, with Trianne, Trinnon, and Magdalena)

;(Subsequent interactions)
**Tribbie:** Ah~! It's Little Gray!
: Miss Tribbie...
**Tribbie:** Hmm? What's up, Little Gray? Got any questions for Miss Tribbie?
:: Is "see you tomorrow" really the greatest prophecy?
**Tribbie:** From what you're saying, I believe it was our past life selves who taught you that. Am I right?
**Tribbie:** We know some people think this isn't a prophecy, but a lie. Because no one can be certain that we'll all meet again tomorrow.
**Tribbie:** However, we still believe in the power of prophecy.
**Tribbie:** When we say "see you tomorrow," it means not only that we hope to meet tomorrow, but also that we'll make an effort to make sure that meeting happens.
**Tribbie:** In other words, "see you tomorrow"... is also a self-fulfilling prophecy!
:: Why do people look forward to tomorrow?
**Tribbie:** We know what you're thinking, Little Gray.
**Tribbie:** Indeed, no one knows what tomorrow will bring, and no one can guarantee that tomorrow will be better.
**Tribbie:** But only until tomorrow will we know what kind of flowers will bloom and what kind of fruits will bear from the "seeds" we planted in the past.
**Tribbie:** Have we become better today than yesterday, and will tomorrow be even better than today?
**Tribbie:** These answers and meanings can only be found as we keep moving forward toward tomorrow!
: (Try to pinch Tribbie's cheeks)
**Tribbie:** Woah! Little Gray! Wh-What are you doing all of a sudden!?
**Trianne:** Wh-What happened?
**Trinnon:** My cheeks... itch a little...
:: Sorry, I just wanted to try that out...
**Tribbie:** Little Gray! You have to show some respect to your teacher! You gave **us** quite a scare, doing that without a warning!
:: So all of you really are interconnected.
**Tribbie:** Of course! I can feel it every time Trianne sneaks some sour snacks!
**Trianne:** Between **us**, there are no secrets!
;(If this option has not been completed yet)
: Is there anything you want me to remember?
:;(Submit )
**Tribbie:** Of course! Ta-da! This is a doll that **we** made by hand. Adorable, isn't it?
**Trianne:** If you ever miss **us**, just give the doll a little squeeze, Little Gray!
**Trinnon:** If you're worried about having nightmares, just cuddle it in your arms.
**Tribbie:** Hehe, **we** hope to be like the stars in the sky, scattered across every sleepless night of yours. And now, you have a star of your very own, Little Gray!
:;(Obtain )
: Let's play again next time!
**Tribbie:** Sure! When Little Gray wants to play, remember to come find **us**!

---
;(In Slumbering Courtyard, with Trianne and Trinnon)
**Tribbie:** Since we don't need to run around delivering prophecies anymore, what should **we** do now? Let's see...
**Trianne:** No more opening the Century Gate... Ugh, everything feels so boring all of a sudden!
**Trinnon:** I'm... so sleepy...
: You could make deliveries.
**Tribbie:** Deliveries? Like, bringing things that people want to them?
**Trinnon:** **We** can use Agy's World Wound Web to find out what everyone wants.
**Trianne:** And then, a whole bunch of **us** can deliver everything!
**Tribbie:** If we're doing that, then **we** need to think of a catchy slogan!
:: You can deliver things with the Century Gate.
**Trianne:** With the Century Gate, we can deliver things instantly, no matter the distance!
**Tribbie:** I've got one! How about... "Knock knock! Tribbie Delivery!"
:: You can launch it over with a rocket.
**Trianne:** Ooh! I've got one for that: "Tribbie lives to deliver!"
**Tribbie:** But that would blow up the packages!
: You could continue teaching.
**Tribbie:** Mmm, I do like all the attentive students in **our** classes.
**Trianne:** Maybe **we** could try teaching different classes!
**Trinnon:** That's true, but what should **we** teach?
:: Lady Tribbie, I want to learn how to do magic tricks.
**Tribbie:** Magic tricks? Oh dear, that's a tough one. **We** have never learned how to do magic tricks ourselves.
**Tribbie:** Why don't you go ask Naxy? I'm sure he knows all sorts of weird and wonderful things.
:: Lady Tribbie, I want to learn how to build rockets.
**Tribbie:** Rockets? Absolutely not... that's way too dangerous.
**Tribbie:** Or are you saying that you just want to see some fireworks? If that's the case, **we** can teach you how to make those, Little Gray.
**Trianne:** Hmm... Doesn't feel like there's much of a difference!
;(If this option has not been completed yet)
: Is there anything you want me to remember?
:;(Submit )
**Tribbie:** Of course! Ta-da! This is a doll that **we** made by hand. Adorable, isn't it?
**Trianne:** If you ever miss **us**, just give the doll a little squeeze, Little Gray!
**Trinnon:** If you're worried about having nightmares, just cuddle it in your arms.
**Tribbie:** Hehe, **we** hope to be like the stars in the sky, scattered across every sleepless night of yours. And now, you have a star of your very own, Little Gray!
:;(Obtain )
: From now on, no more goodbyes.
**Trinnon & Tribbie & Trianne:** Yes! We'll all be together, not just tomorrow, but forever and ever and ever!
